## Review

**8 out of 10, Strong.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck is a disciplined, consistent system: one dark template, a fixed title and orange subtitle pattern, a restrained palette, readable direct labels and source lines on every chart. Its main technique is progressive build, as in slides 7 to 11 where one stacked bar gains a segment per slide, and slides 32 to 34 where bubbles are added one group at a time. This lets a speaker walk an audience through one idea at a time, and the section dividers and the opening map on slide 5 help the route. The weaknesses are that the many near-identical build slides make the 106-page length hard to scan, several charts leave large empty areas (slide 8), and tiny labels and footers would be hard to read in a big room.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent template, action-style titles over orange unit subtitles, direct labelling, the one-element-per-slide builds, and the orange highlight for the focus item.

**Most useful repair:** Raise the smallest segment labels and footer text, fill or rebalance the empty right half on single-bar slides such as slide 8, and add takeaway titles to the plain 'The spread of...' and 'Global company creation' slides.

**What would change the score:** I would merge some of the repeated build steps (for example slides 44 to 47 where the same two empty circles recur) into fewer slides, and add an agenda or progress marker so the 106-page route is easier to follow.

_Confidence medium. 72 of 106 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

Benedict Evans, an independent analyst and former a16z partner, gave a long talk on technology markets in November 2018. It opens with a title slide and an 'The end of the beginning' thesis, noting that most people are online while most of the money is not. The main sections follow in order. Market sizes examine ecommerce as a share of US retail spending, along with restaurants, gas stations, cars and advertising. New problems describe the internet moving from easy, low-capital categories to harder ones such as groceries, housing, transport, finance and health. Packets versus preference contrasts retail as logistics with retail as tastemaking. New layers cover machine learning and crypto as the next organising systems after search and social. The deck closes with a repeated 'The end of the beginning' section break, a 'What is this?' slide and a thank you slide.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Benedict Evans deck have?**

The Benedict Evans deck has 106 pages, and its title slide, 'The End of the Beginning', is slide 1. The deck is presented in the order described on its opening slides.

**What does the Benedict Evans deck say about ecommerce's share of US retail spending?**

Slide 8 is headed 'But still only just reaching 10% of US retail spending', and its chart labels ecommerce at 9%. The chart uses 2017 US retail spending in trillions of dollars.

**What does the Benedict Evans deck say about selling groceries online?**

Slide 53, 'New problems: groceries', says groceries 'breaks the Amazon model' and 'can't be bolted onto a commodity logistics platform', so it 'needs a whole new platform'. The same slide adds that the market is 'so big it's worth it'.

**What does the Benedict Evans deck say about machine learning and crypto?**

Slide 5 lists them under 'New structural layers' with the text 'After search & social, now ML and Crypto'. The slide presents them as the next layers after search and social.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Review: This lets a speaker walk an audience through one idea at a time, and the section dividers and the opening map on slide 5 help the route.

**Text**

Most people / are online…
But most of the / money is not
New problems
Tackling harder / markets and / changing them / more
New structural / layers
After search & / social, now ML / and Crypto
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 4

**Layout**

Three equal circles sit in one horizontal row across the vertical middle of the slide, with the left circle centred near x 15%, the middle near x 49% and the right near x 83%. Each circle holds a centred bold heading over a centred regular body text block. No slide title sits above the row. A small copyright and page line sits bottom-left at about 3% from the left edge and 4% from the bottom.

**Visuals**

Three outlined circles, left to right: an orange ring (about #E0822A), a steel-blue ring (about #6B97B4) and a magenta ring (about #C4417F). Each ring is about 2.5 mm stroke, i.e. about 0.3% of slide height, with no fill. Between the left and middle circles, and between the middle and right circles, is a grey dotted double-headed arrow (about #A8ACB0) with filled triangular heads at both ends touching the ring edges at the vertical centre of the circles. Each arrow is about 8.6% of slide width long and about 1.8% of slide height tall. No photographs, icons or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F (dark slate grey) covering the entire slide. Flat dark slate background (#40484F) with no gradient, texture or shadows. All text is white (#FFFFFF). Headings are Proxima Nova Bold (geometric sans-serif) at about 4.4% of slide height; body text is Proxima Nova Regular at about 4.0% of slide height, set in centred lines with normal leading. Accent colours are the three ring strokes: orange #E0822A, steel blue #6B97B4 and magenta #C4417F. Connectors are light grey #A8ACB0 dotted lines with solid triangular arrowheads.

- Review: Its main technique is progressive build, as in slides 7 to 11 where one stacked bar gains a segment per slide, and slides 32 to 34 where bubbles are added one group at a time.

**Text**

Ecommerce is big in dollar terms
Quarterly US ecommerce revenue ($bn)
150
100
50
0
Mar 99
Mar 02
Mar 05
Mar 08
Mar 11

Mar 17
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 6
Source: BLS

**Layout**

Single full-bleed chart slide with no columns or grid. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top and 3.4% from the left, with an orange subtitle directly beneath it. A line chart fills the middle and lower area, with its y-axis labels at the left and its x-axis baseline and year labels along the bottom. The footer carries the copyright line at bottom-left and the source line at bottom-right, both at about 95.5% from the top. There is no slide number; the footer reads 'Page 6' on a slide numbered 7.

**Visuals**

Line chart. Title: none inside the chart; the slide title is 'Ecommerce is big in dollar terms' and the subtitle is 'Quarterly US ecommerce revenue ($bn)'. Y-axis: labelled 0, 50, 100, 150, units $bn, range 0 to 150, no axis title. X-axis: time, quarterly, labelled Mar 99, Mar 02, Mar 05, Mar 08, Mar 11, Mar 14, Mar 17, with ticks every three years; the series runs from about Mar 99 to about late 2018, with no axis title. Series: one orange line, no legend, no printed data values. Estimated values (about): starts about 5 at Mar 99, about 10 at Mar 01, about 20 at Mar 04, about 30 at Mar 07, about 40 at Mar 09, about 55 at Mar 11, about 70 at Mar 13, about 85 at Mar 15, about 105 at Mar 16, about 122 at Mar 17, a peak of about 141 just after Mar 17, a drop to about 112, then about 120 at the final point. The line shows a strong seasonal zigzag with a spike each fourth quarter. Highlighted points: none. Footer items are rendered as two raster images: copyright text at bottom-left and 'Source: BLS' at bottom-right. No logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat slate #40484F. Flat dark slate background with no gradient, texture or photograph. Title in white (#FFFFFF) ProximaNova-Light, sans-serif, light weight, about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. Subtitle in amber (#FCB316) ProximaNova-Light, about 4% of slide height. Chart tick labels in white (#FFFFFF) ProximaNova-Regular, about 3% of slide height. Chart line in orange, about #E07F26, roughly 0.5% of slide height thick, with rounded joins. Horizontal x-axis baseline in white (#FFFFFF), thin, about 0.2% of height. Footer text small, white, about 1.2% of height. No shapes, corner radii, shadows or boxes.

### Slide 8

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A stacked bar builds up one segment at a time across slides 7 to 11, so the viewer sees how the ecommerce share is put in context.
- Review: Its main technique is progressive build, as in slides 7 to 11 where one stacked bar gains a segment per slide, and slides 32 to 34 where bubbles are added one group at a time.
- Review: The weaknesses are that the many near-identical build slides make the 106-page length hard to scan, several charts leave large empty areas (slide 8), and tiny labels and footers would be hard to read in a big room.
- Suggested repair: Raise the smallest segment labels and footer text, fill or rebalance the empty right half on single-bar slides such as slide 8, and add takeaway titles to the plain 'The spread of...' and 'Global company creation' slides.

**Text**

But still only just reaching 10% of US retail spending / US retail spending, 2017 ($tr) / 6 / 5 / 4 / 3 / 2 / 1 / 0 / Physical retail / Ecommerce / 9% / Retail / Consumer Spending / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 7 / Source: BLS

**Layout**

Single-column title block at top left: the headline sits at the top margin and the orange subtitle is directly beneath it, both left-aligned to about 3.4% of slide width. One stacked column chart occupies the left third, with its y-axis labels at about 4.7% x and its single category label beneath the baseline. The right two-thirds of the slide is empty. The footer runs along the bottom margin, with the copyright and page number image at left (x 2.9%) and the Source: BLS image at right (x 74.8%).

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart, single column, no chart title inside the chart area (the title is the slide headline). Y-axis: unit is $tr (trillions of US dollars), ticks labelled 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, range 0 to 6, gridlines none, axis labels white. X-axis: one category labelled Retail, baseline is a white horizontal line. Series 1 'Physical retail' (blue, #6C96B2), top of segment about 5.05 $tr on the axis, label 'Physical retail' printed inside the segment in white. Series 2 'Ecommerce' (orange, #D98228), sits on the baseline, top about 0.45 $tr (about estimate, no value printed), label 'Ecommerce' printed inside the segment in white. Highlighted value: '9%' printed in white to the right of the column top of the orange segment, indicating ecommerce share of total retail. No legend. No points highlighted beyond the 9% label. Column spans about 17.1% to 30.3% of slide width. Two rendered images in footer: a 194 by 26 pixel copyright/page text image and a 76 by 27 pixel Source: BLS image; no logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F dark slate grey. Flat dark slate background with no gradient or texture. Headline in ProximaNova-Light, white #FFFFFF, roughly 5.9% of slide height, sans-serif, normal case. Subtitle in ProximaNova-Light, amber #FCB316, roughly 4.0% of slide height. Axis numbers and category labels in ProximaNova-Regular, white #FFFFFF, roughly 3.0% of slide height. Segment labels in ProximaNova-Regular, white, roughly 2.5% of slide height. Footer text is small, about 1.5% of slide height, white-grey. Bars are flat fills with no outline, square corners, no shadow. Baseline is a thin white line about 0.2% of slide height thick.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#40484F]: Full-slide background rectangle (Rectangle covering the whole slide, drawn over a white #FFFFFF fill of the same size beneath it so only the dark slate shows. No outline, no shadow.)
- shape at x 17.1, y 34.3, w 13.2, h 42.7 [#6C96B2]: Column segment for Physical retail, blue (Flat rectangle, square corners, no outline, no shadow, sits on top of the orange segment.)

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Review: Its main technique is progressive build, as in slides 7 to 11 where one stacked bar gains a segment per slide, and slides 32 to 34 where bubbles are added one group at a time.

**Text**

Now add restaurants….
US retail spending, 2017 ($tr)
6
5
Bars & restaurants
4
3
2
Physical retail
1
0
Retail
Ecommerce
8%
Source: BLS
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 8

**Layout**

A single stacked column chart sits in the left portion of the slide, with its y-axis labels at the left and the category axis line running horizontally beneath the bar. The title is top-left at about 3.4% from the left and 6% from the top, with the yellow subtitle directly beneath it; the right two-thirds of the slide is empty. The footer runs along the bottom margin, with the copyright line at the far left and the 'Source: BLS' line at about 75% across.

**Visuals**

Chart: a single stacked vertical column chart, category 'Retail', on a y-axis from 0 to 6 with no unit suffix (the unit $tr is given in the subtitle). Segments from bottom to top: Ecommerce, about 0.4, orange #D98228; Physical retail, about 4.6, blue-grey #6C96B2; Bars & restaurants, about 0.7, magenta #BE4681; the column totals about 5.8. The Ecommerce segment is labelled '8%' to its right, as printed. Segment labels are printed inside each segment in white. No legend, no gridlines, no tick marks other than the axis labels 0 to 6. The white horizontal baseline runs from about x 7.2% to 42.4% at y 81%. No highlighted points. A pale-grey rectangle filled #40484F sits over the area around 'Consumer Spending' and hides that text. No photographs, icons or logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F (dark slate grey). Flat dark slate background #40484F with no gradient or texture. Title text is white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Light (geometric humanist sans-serif, light weight), about 5.9% of slide height for the title and 4.0% for the subtitle. Subtitle colour is amber #FCB316. Chart labels are white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Regular at about 3.0% for axis numbers and category label and 2.5% for segment labels. Segment fills are flat with no borders or shadows: orange #D98228, steel blue #6C96B2, magenta #BE4681. The baseline is a 1 to 2px white line. Footer is light grey-white text on the dark background, small at about 1.2% of slide height. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Review: Its main technique is progressive build, as in slides 7 to 11 where one stacked bar gains a segment per slide, and slides 32 to 34 where bubbles are added one group at a time.

**Text**

Gas stations

©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 9
Source: BLS

**Layout**

Single-column chart on a dark slate field. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top with a yellow subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned to a margin of about 3.4% from the left edge. One stacked column sits at the left third of the slide, with its y-axis numbers to its left and its baseline running to about 42% of the width. The right 55% of the slide is empty. The footer line holds the copyright text at bottom-left and 'Source: BLS' at bottom-right, both near 95.5% from the top.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart, single category 'Retail'. Y-axis: unlabelled axis with tick values 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6 (units $tr, shown only in subtitle), range 0 to 6. X-axis: category 'Retail', baseline a white horizontal line. Series, stacked bottom to top, with labels printed inside segments and no printed values: Ecommerce (orange #D98228), about 0.4 $tr; Gas stations (purple #773F9B), about 0.5 $tr; Physical retail (steel blue #6C96B2), about 4.1 $tr; Bars & restaurants (magenta #BE4681), about 0.7 $tr; total about 5.7 $tr. No legend; segment labels serve as legend. No highlighted points. The text 'Consumer Spending' is present in the file but is covered by a background-coloured rectangle (#40484F) and is not visible.

**Design**

Background flat dark slate #40484F. Titles and body text white #FFFFFF; subtitle and accent in amber #FCB316. Typeface ProximaNova: Light for title and subtitle, Regular for axis and segment labels, sans-serif, geometric. Title about 5.9% of slide height, subtitle about 4.0%, labels about 2.5%, axis numbers about 3.0%. Chart segments are flat filled rectangles with no outlines, no corner radius, no shadows and no gradients. Baseline is a thin white line about 0.2% high.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: Its main technique is progressive build, as in slides 7 to 11 where one stacked bar gains a segment per slide, and slides 32 to 34 where bubbles are added one group at a time.

**Text**

And the $1.2tr spent on cars / US retail spending, 2017 ($tr) / 6 / 5 / Bars & restaurants / 4 / 3 / Physical retail / 2 / 1 / Cars & parts / 0 / Gas stations / Ecommerce / Retail / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 10 / Source: BLS

**Layout**

A single stacked column chart sits in the left-centre of the slide, with its y-axis numbers at the left edge of the chart and the category label 'Retail' centred beneath the column. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the left and 6% from the top, with the subtitle directly beneath it. The right half of the slide is empty. The copyright and page line sits bottom-left and the source line sits bottom-right at about 75% across, both on a shared baseline near 96% of the height.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked vertical column chart, single category 'Retail', y-axis 0 to 6 in $tr, no axis title printed, no gridlines, no legend, no printed value labels. Segments from bottom to top: Ecommerce (orange #D98228) about 0.4; Gas stations (purple #773F9B) about 0.5; Cars & parts (green #95CA53) about 1.2; Physical retail (blue #6C96B2) about 3.0; Bars & restaurants (magenta #BE4681) about 0.7; total about 5.8. Segment names are printed in white inside each segment. No segment is highlighted. Column spans about 17.1% to 30.3% across the slide. Horizontal white baseline at the zero line. Footer images: copyright line and source line as small raster images.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat fill #40484F dark slate. Flat dark slate background #40484F with no gradient or texture. Title in white #FFFFFF ProximaNova-Light, a geometric sans-serif, large, about 5.9% of slide height with normal case. Subtitle in amber #FCB316 ProximaNova-Light, about 4.0% of slide height. Axis numbers and segment labels in white #FFFFFF ProximaNova-Regular, about 3.0% of height for axis numbers and about 2.5% for segment labels. Segments are flat-filled rectangles with no borders, shadows or rounded corners. Baseline is a thin white line of about 1 px. Footer text is small, light grey-white and sans-serif.

**Layout**

Single-column chart slide with a left-aligned title block at the top left (title at about x 3.4%, y 6.1%; amber subtitle directly beneath it at y 13.3%). A stacked-bar chart fills the middle, with the y-axis labels at the far left and the horizontal baseline running from about x 7% to 73%. The footer sits along the bottom edge: the copyright and page number at the bottom left (x 2.9%, y 95.5%) and the source line at bottom centre-right (x 74.8%, y 95.5%). Right side of the slide beyond x 73% is empty.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart. Title: none inside the chart; slide subtitle reads 'US spending, 2017 ($tr)'. Y axis: 0 to 15 in $ trillions, labelled 0, 5, 10, 15 at the left, no gridlines, no axis title. X axis: two categories, 'Retail' and 'Consumer Spending', with a thin white baseline. No legend; the segments are unlabelled. Retail column (about 5.7 total): bottom orange segment about 0 to 0.5, then grey segments about 0.5 to 3.4, 3.4 to 5.1 and 5.1 to 5.7 (all estimates, no values printed). Consumer Spending column (about 12.0 total): bottom blue segment about 0 to 1.3, then about 1.3 to 4.4, about 4.4 to 5.8, and about 5.8 to 12.0 (all estimates, no values printed). Thin darker separator lines divide the segments. No highlighted points. Footer and source are small text images, not photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F (dark slate grey). Dark slate background #40484F, flat with no gradient. Title text white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova-Light (geometric sans-serif, light weight), about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle amber #FCB316 in ProximaNova-Light, about 3.9% of slide height. Axis numbers and category labels white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova-Regular, about 3.0% of slide height. Retail bar: orange #D9822B base segment and grey #838586 upper segments with thin #6A6D6F separators. Consumer Spending bar: steel blue #6C96B2 segments with thin darker #4F6F84 separators. Baseline is a 1px white #FFFFFF line. Bars are flat rectangles with square corners, no shadows, no outlines beyond the separators. Footer text light grey #C8CBCE at very small size.

### Slide 13

**Text**

What’s the opportunity?
US spending, 2017 ($tr)
15
10
5
0
4%
Retail
Consumer Spending
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 12
Source: BLS

**Layout**

Single-slide chart layout on a flat dark background. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6% from the top, with the subtitle directly beneath it. A vertical value axis with labels 0, 5, 10 and 15 sits at the left of the chart area, and a horizontal baseline runs across the lower chart area with two category labels centred below it. The footer copyright line sits bottom-left at about 3% from the left edge and 96% from the top, and the source line sits bottom-right of the chart area at about 75% from the left edge. There is no slide number element other than the 'Page 12' text in the footer.

**Visuals**

Stacked bar chart, untitled apart from the slide subtitle 'US spending, 2017 ($tr)'. The value axis runs from 0 to 15 ($tr), with labels at 0, 5, 10 and 15 and no visible gridlines. Two categories: 'Retail' and 'Consumer Spending'. Retail is a single stacked bar, about 5.7 $tr high (top segment boundary at about 5.0 and 3.4, the lower grey segment top at about 0.4), with a thin orange base segment labelled '4%' printed to the right of the bar top-right corner. Its segments are grey #838586 with thin darker separator lines, except the bottom segment which is orange (about #DB822B). Consumer Spending is a single stacked bar about 12 $tr high, with grey #838586 segments and thin darker separator lines at about 1.3, 4.4 and 5.8 $tr (estimated, no values printed). No legend. No segment values other than the '4%' label are printed. No highlighted points beyond the orange Retail base segment. Baseline is a white horizontal line at the zero level, about 2px thick.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F covering the full slide. Dark slate background #40484F with flat fill and no gradient or texture. Title text white #FFFFFF in a light geometric sans-serif (ProximaNova Light), large. Subtitle in amber-yellow #FCB316, light sans-serif (ProximaNova Light), about two thirds the title size. Axis labels and category labels are white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Regular, about 3% of slide height. The '4%' label is white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Light, about 2.5% of slide height. Bars are flat grey #838586 with orange #DB822B accent on the Retail base. Baseline is a white #FFFFFF line. No shadows, no rounded corners, no borders on bars. Footer and source text are small (about 2.5% of slide height), white, ProximaNova Light.

**Layout**

Single-slide chart layout on a full-bleed dark slate background. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top with a coloured subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned to a left margin of about 3.4%. The chart's plot area spans roughly x 7% to 74% and y 22% to 81%, with the x-axis baseline at about 81% of the height. Series end labels sit in a column at about x 74.5% to the right of the plot. The footer runs along the bottom at about y 95.5%, with the copyright and page number left-aligned at the left margin and the source line left-aligned at about x 74.8%.

**Visuals**

Chart: multi-series line chart, not stacked. Title: Digital advertising also looks big… Subtitle: Global advertising, 2018 dollars ($bn). Y axis: $bn, 0 to 250, gridlines none, tick labels 0, 50, 100, 150, 200, 250. X axis: years 1985 to 2018, labelled every five years (1985, 1990, 1995, 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015), with the 2018 endpoint unlabelled. Four series, each a 3-4 px stroke with rounded joins, labelled at the right end: Internet (orange #E07F27), TV (magenta #C64A84), Print (steel blue #6A95B5), All other (green #8DC63F). Values are estimates marked about since the slide prints none. Internet: about 0 from 1985 to 1996, about 10 in 2000, about 27 in 2005, about 61 in 2009, about 110 in 2013, about 180 in 2016, about 223 in 2018. TV: about 78 in 1985, about 101 in 1990, about 162 in 2000, about 160 in 2009, about 190 in 2014, about 183 in 2018. Print: about 135 in 1985, about 168 in 1990, about 215 at its peak in 2000, about 197 in 2007, about 139 in 2010, about 100 in 2015, about 73 in 2018. All other: about 33 in 1985, about 69 in 2000, about 78 in 2007, about 66 in 2009, about 73 in 2018. Legend: none; series are identified by end labels. Highlighted points: none. Axis baseline is a white horizontal rule at 0.

**Design**

Background flat dark slate #40484F with no gradient or texture. Title text is white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Light (geometric humanist sans-serif, light weight), sentence case, approximately 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle is amber #FCB316 in ProximaNova Light, about 4.0% of slide height. Axis tick labels are white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Regular, about 3.0% of slide height. Series end labels are white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Light, about 2.5% of slide height. Footer and source lines are white ProximaNova Regular at about 1.5% of slide height. Line and shape styles: series lines solid, no markers, no shadows, no corner radii. Axis baseline is a thin white rule. No gradients.

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a full-bleed dark slate background. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge, with the orange subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on one margin. A single stacked column sits in the left third of the slide, with a y-axis of value labels at its left and a horizontal baseline beneath it; the right two-thirds of the slide is empty. The footer runs along the bottom edge: the copyright line with page number at bottom-left and the source line at about 75% across. No slide-number box is separate from the footer text.

**Visuals**

Chart: a single stacked column chart. Type: vertical stacked column, one column. Title: none inside the chart; the subtitle above reads 'US advertising, 2018 ($bn)'. Y-axis: linear, $bn implied by the subtitle, range 0 to 250, labelled ticks at 0, 50, 100, 150, 200 and 250, no axis title. X-axis: one unlabelled category, drawn as a white horizontal baseline. Series, bottom to top: Internet about 0 to 78 (about $78bn, orange segment, labelled 'Internet' inside); Print about 78 to 106 (about $28bn, blue-grey segment, labelled 'Print' inside); TV about 106 to 175 (about $69bn, magenta segment, labelled 'TV' inside); Other media about 175 to 203 (about $28bn, green segment, labelled 'Other media' inside). Total about 203, shown by the column top at the 200 gridline. Beside the Internet segment, outside the column on the right, the printed value '38%' appears, marking Internet's share. No legend; no gridlines; no highlighted points beyond the 38% label. Column width about 13.2% of slide width, centred over the axis at about x 17.5 to 30.7%. Segment colours: Internet #D98228, Print #6C96B2, TV #BE4681, Other media #95CA53, all with thin no-stroke edges. Segment labels are white, centred horizontally in each segment. Footer contains two small images: one 194 by 26 pixel image spanning the copyright line at bottom-left, and one 88 by 27 pixel image at bottom-right under 'Source: Zenith'; a 51 by 52 pixel image sits at about x 14.8%, y 95.5%, adjacent to the copyright line, and its content is not clearly legible.

**Design**

Background solid slate grey #40484F, flat with no gradient, texture or image. Titles are white #FFFFFF; subtitle is amber #FCB316; body and chart labels are white #FFFFFF; footer text is light grey-white, about #D8DCE0. Typeface is Proxima Nova: Light for the title and the 38% label, Regular for axis numbers and segment labels. Title size about 5.9% of slide height (cap height about 4.1%); subtitle about 3.95%; axis labels and segment labels about 2.5 to 3.0%; footer text is very small. Lines: the baseline is a 1-point-equivalent white horizontal rule. Segments are rectangles with square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

Source: Galbi

**Layout**

Single full-width chart slide on a dark slate ground. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the left and 6% from the top, with a yellow subtitle directly beneath it. The line chart occupies the middle band from about 25% to 85% of the height, with its x-axis baseline spanning roughly 7% to 73% of the width, leaving the right quarter empty. Y-axis labels are left-aligned at x 3.5%, and year labels sit under the baseline. A footer row at about 95.5% of the height holds the copyright line at left and the source line at about 75% across. There is no slide-number element beyond the ‘Page 15’ text in the footer.

**Visuals**

Chart: line chart. Title: US advertising as % US GDP (rendered as the yellow subtitle). Y-axis: US advertising as a percentage of US GDP, unit %, labelled at 0%, 1%, 2%, 3%, 4%, range 0% to 4%, no gridlines. X-axis: calendar years, unit year, labelled every ten years 1920 to 2000 (last plotted point about 2007, not labelled). Single series, a continuous line in steel blue #6B93B0 about 0.5% of slide height thick with square-cornered joins. Estimated values, all about: 1920 about 2.8%; 1922 about 3.0% (local peak); 1930 about 2.7%; 1933 about 2.3%; 1934 about 2.5%; 1941 about 2.1%; 1942 about 1.3%; 1944 about 1.2% (lowest point, trough); 1950 about 1.9%; 1956 about 2.3%; 1960 about 2.3%; 1966 about 2.1%; 1970 about 1.8%; 1975 about 1.7%; 1980 about 1.9%; 1987 about 2.3%; 1992 about 2.1%; 1997 about 2.3%; 2000 about 2.5% (highest after 1922); 2007 about 2.0%, the last point. Marketing is a single grey #838586 floating column from about 203 to about 438 (estimated, no printed value), not stacked on a baseline. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the 18% label. ### Slide 19

**Text**

Reaching customers / Old definitions, new use cases / Pay for placement on / Amazon / “Advertising” / Pay for placement in / Walmart / ”Marketing" / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 18

**Layout**

Single-slide diagram on a dark background: title and subtitle are left-aligned at the top-left margin (about 3.4% from the left edge), two equal circles sit side by side across the middle of the slide with a double-headed dotted arrow between them, and a small copyright footer sits bottom-left with the page number at its right end. No visible slide-number box beyond the footer text; the page number reads 18.

**Visuals**

Diagram: two outlined circles of equal size and a double-headed arrow connecting them. Left circle: orange outline (about #D9822B), stroke about 0.4% of slide height, centred around x 36%, y 49%, diameter about 46% of slide height (about x 7% to 43.5%, y 24.7% to 89.2%); it contains the bold label 'Pay for placement on Amazon' and the quoted word '“Advertising”'. Right circle: steel-blue outline (about #5E8FAF), same size, centred around x 74.5%, y 49%, spanning about x 56.5% to 93%, y 24.7% to 89.2%; it contains the bold label 'Pay for placement in Walmart' and the quoted word '”Marketing"'. Between the circles: a grey (about #A8ADB2) double-headed arrow with filled triangular heads, dotted shaft, running horizontally at y about 56.7%, from about x 43.5% to 56.2%. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid charcoal slate #40484F covering the whole slide.

### Slide 20

**Text**

And what do you really pay to reach a customer? / US budgets, 2018 ($bn) / 700 / 600 / Retail rents? / 500 / 400 / Shipping? / 300 / 200 / 100 / 0 / Advertising / Marketing / Logistics / Internet / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 19 / Source: Zenith, BLS, Pitney Bowes, a16z

**Layout**

Single-slide chart layout on a full-bleed dark slate background. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top with a left margin of about 3.4%, and the orange subtitle sits directly beneath it. The chart fills the middle band: a vertical value axis with labels 0 to 700 at the left (about x 3 to 5%), a horizontal white baseline spanning about x 7.6 to 73.6% at y 80.9%, and three category columns (Advertising, Marketing, Logistics) centred at about x 18.6%, 41.8% and 63.7%. The footer runs along the bottom at about y 95.5%: copyright and page number bottom-left, source line bottom-right ending at about x 89%. No slide number other than the 'Page 19' footer text; no logo of note; no legend.

**Visuals**

Chart: floating stacked-column chart (bars floating above a zero baseline, with grey segments). Y-axis: US dollars in billions ($bn), linear scale from 0 to 700 with labelled ticks every 100 (0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600, 700), white labels. X-axis: three categories labelled Advertising, Marketing, Logistics, no axis title. Title: 'US budgets, 2018 ($bn)' as subtitle. No legend. Series and labelled values: Advertising column consists of an orange segment labelled 'Internet' from 0 to about 80 (estimate, no value printed) and a grey segment stacked above it up to about 200 (about, no value printed); the grey segment has two faint darker horizontal divider lines inside it. Marketing column is a single grey floating segment from about 200 to about 440 (about, no value printed) with faint divider lines inside it at about 250, 300 and 350 (about). Logistics column consists of a blue segment labelled 'Shipping?' from about 440 to about 545 (about) and a magenta segment labelled 'Retail rents?' from about 545 to about 695 (about). Highlighted points: the orange Internet segment, the blue Shipping segment and the magenta Retail rents segment are the only coloured segments, the grey segments are unlabelled. Image elements: a 215 by 27 pixel raster of the source line 'Source: Zenith, BLS, Pitney Bowes, a16z' at bottom right; a 194 by 26 pixel raster of the copyright line '©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 19' at bottom left; and a small 51 by 52 pixel raster at about x 14.8%, y 95.5%, overlapping the end of the copyright line (likely the page number digits '19'), not a photograph. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat slate colour #40484F across the whole slide. Flat dark slate background, no gradients or textures. Title in Proxima Nova Light (geometric humanist sans-serif, light weight), white #FFFFFF, about 5.9% of slide height (cap height about 4.2%). Subtitle in Proxima Nova Light, amber/yellow #FCB316, about 4.0% of slide height. Axis labels and category labels in Proxima Nova Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 3.0% of slide height. Bar labels in Proxima Nova Regular white about 2.5% of slide height. Footer text is small (about 1.3% of height) light grey-white, Proxima Nova. Bars are flat fills with no outlines or shadows; grey segments #838586 have faint darker divider lines; the baseline is a 1 px white line; no rounded corners; no shadows.

### Slide 21

**Text**

Globally, $1tr is spent on “what should I buy?”
Global sales & marketing, 2018 ($tr)
1.00
Events
0.75
Direct
0.50
Other advertising
TV
0.25
Print
0.00
Internet
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 20
Source: GroupM

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a left-aligned title block at the top and one stacked column chart occupying the left third, with its baseline running right. The right two-thirds of the slide is empty. The title sits at the top-left with the subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned to about 3.4% margin. The footer copyright line sits bottom-left and the source line sits bottom-right of centre, both on a single baseline near 96.6% of slide height; there is no slide number element apart from 'Page 20' inside the copyright line.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked single-column bar chart, vertical. Title: none inside the chart; the slide title and subtitle 'Global sales & marketing, 2018 ($tr)' serve as its title. Y-axis: linear, unit $tr, range 0.00 to 1.00, tick labels 0.00, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00 at left, no gridlines. X-axis: a single unlabelled category, drawn as a white horizontal baseline. Series, stacked from bottom to top with labelled values where the slide prints none (values are estimates from segment heights, marked about): Internet about 0.22 (#D98228, orange); Print about 0.08 (#6C96B2, steel blue); TV about 0.20 (#BE4681, magenta); Other advertising about 0.08 (#95CA53, green); Direct about 0.35 (#FCB316, amber); Events about 0.07 (#773F9B, purple). Segments sum to about 1.00. Each segment carries its name in white centred text. Legend: none, segments are labelled directly. Highlighted points: none. Column width about 13.2% of slide width, centred at about 24.5% of width. No photographs, illustrations, logos or icons are visible. Four placed images exist in the file (194 by 26 px, 112 by 53 px, 112 by 53 px and 57 by 52 px) but are not visible in the render and are treated as hidden.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate grey #40484F. Flat dark slate background with no gradient, texture or photograph. Titles are white light-weight geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova Light) at about 5.9% of slide height, body and axis labels are white regular-weight Proxima Nova at about 3.0% of height, segment labels are about 2.5% of height. The subtitle is amber #FCB316 at about 4.0% of height. Chart colours are saturated flat fills with no outlines, no shadows, no rounded corners. The baseline is a thin white line about 0.2% of height. ### Slide 23

The spread of industrialisation
Global manufacturing production
75%
50%
25%
USA
UK
0%
1830

1913
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 22
Source: WTO

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a full-bleed dark grey field. Title sits top-left at about x 3.4% and y 6.1%, with the yellow subtitle directly beneath it at y 13.3%. A stacked column chart occupies the left-centre of the slide, spanning roughly x 3% to 58% and y 24% to 86%, with its baseline at y 81%. The right side of the slide (x 58% to 100%) is empty. The footer runs along y 95.5%, with the copyright and page line at the left margin and the source line at x 74.8%. There is no slide number other than 'Page 22' in the copyright line.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart, no chart title. Y axis labelled 0%, 25%, 50%, 75% (percent of global manufacturing production; range 0% to 75%, gridlines absent). X axis categories 1830, 1860, 1913 with the baseline drawn as a thin white horizontal line. Each column is split into two series: UK in orange #D98228 at the bottom and USA in blue #6C96B2 on top. Values are not printed, so estimates are given: 1830 total about 12% (UK about 9%, USA about 3%); 1860 total about 27% (UK about 20%, USA about 7%); 1913 total about 46% (UK about 13%, USA about 32%). Series labels are printed inside the 1913 columns: 'USA' in the blue segment and 'UK' in the orange segment. No legend and no highlighted points. Columns are about 6.5% of slide width wide. A grey box #40484F sits over the right part of the chart area, hiding text that is not visible in the rendered slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat dark slate grey #40484F, full-bleed.

### Slide 24

**Text**

The spread of industrialisation / Global manufacturing production / 75% / 50% / USA / 25% / UK / 0% / 1830 / 1860 / 1913 / 2015 / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 23 / Source: WTO

**Layout**

Single-column chart slide on a full-bleed dark background. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top, with an orange subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned to a left margin of about 3.4%. A stacked column chart occupies the middle and lower area, starting at about x 7.8% and ending at x 73.8% on a baseline at about y 81.1%. The footer runs along the bottom at about y 95.5%: copyright and page number at left, and 'Source: WTO' at about x 75%. No slide number is printed separately beyond the footer's 'Page 23'.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart. Title: none on the chart itself. Y-axis: percentage of global manufacturing production, 0% to 75%, tick labels at 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, no gridlines. X-axis: years 1830, 1860, 1913, 2015, no axis title. Two series stacked per column: UK in orange (#D98228, bottom) and USA in blue-grey (#6C96B2, top). No printed values on the bars; values are estimates: 1830 UK about 9%, USA about 3% (total about 12%); 1860 UK about 20%, USA about 7% (total about 27%); 1913 UK about 13.5%, USA about 32% (total about 46%); 2015 UK about 2%, USA about 18% (total about 20%). Series are labelled inline: 'UK' in white inside the orange segment of 1913, and 'USA' in white inside the blue segment of 1913. No legend. No highlighted points. Baseline is a thin white horizontal line at 0%.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat fill #40484F dark slate. Dark slate background with a flat fill, no gradient or texture. Title in light-weight geometric sans-serif (ProximaNova-Light style) in white #FFFFFF, roughly 5.9% of slide height for the cap-height-inclusive line. Subtitle in the same light sans-serif in amber #FCB316, about 4% of slide height. Axis and category labels in regular-weight sans-serif in white #FFFFFF, about 3% of slide height. Inline series labels are about 2.5% of slide height in white. Footer and source text are small, about 1.5% of slide height, light grey-white. Bars are flat fills with no outline, no shadows, no rounded corners. Baseline is a white line about 0.2% of slide height thick.

### Slide 25

The spread of industrialisation
Global manufacturing production

©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 24
Source: WTO

**Layout**

Single-slide stacked column chart on a full-bleed dark slate background. The title is top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6.1% from the top, with the yellow subtitle directly beneath it at about 13.3%. The chart sits in the middle band with the y-axis labels in a column at the left (about 3% from the left edge), a horizontal baseline at about 81% of the height running from about 7.8% to 73.6% of the width, and category labels 1913 and 2015 centred under their bars. The footer runs along the bottom at about 95.5% of the height, with the copyright line at the left and 'Source: WTO' at about 75% from the left edge. There is no slide number apart from 'Page 24' in the footer text, and no right-hand panel.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart. Title: none (the slide title serves as the chart heading). Subtitle: Global manufacturing production. Y-axis: percentage of global manufacturing production, linear, 0% to 75%, labelled at 0%, 25%, 50% and 75%, with no gridlines. X-axis: two categories, 1913 and 2015. Series, bottom to top: UK (orange #D98228) and USA (steel blue #6C96B2). Printed values: none; the segments carry only the labels UK and USA, shown on the 1913 bar. Estimated values (about): 1913 UK about 13.5%, 1913 USA about 32% (bar top about 46%); 2015 UK about 2%, 2015 USA about 18% (bar top about 20%). Legend: none; series are identified by in-bar labels. Highlighted points: none. Bars are flat-filled with no outline, about 13% of slide width each. Baseline: white horizontal line at 0%. Two small images at the footer, one about 13% wide and one about 5% wide, plus a small image about 2% wide, are not legible at this resolution and are not identifiable as logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F (dark slate grey). Flat dark slate background #40484F with no gradient or texture. Title in ProximaNova-Light (Proxima Nova Light, a geometric sans-serif), regular weight, white #FFFFFF, about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in ProximaNova-Light, about 4.0% of slide height, amber/yellow #FCB316. Axis labels and category labels in ProximaNova-Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 3.0% of slide height. In-bar labels USA and UK in ProximaNova-Regular, white, about 2.5% of slide height. Footer text in small white sans-serif, under 1% of slide height. Accents: orange #D98228 for UK and blue #6C96B2 for USA. Baseline is a thin white line. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

The right fifth of the slide is empty.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart. No chart title beyond the slide title. Y axis: linear percentage scale, labelled 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, with tick labels at the left of the chart and no gridlines; the range is 0% to about 75%. X axis: two categories, 1913 and 2015, labelled beneath the baseline. Each column is stacked with three series, bottom to top: UK (orange #D98228), USA (blue #6C96B2), China (magenta #BE4681). Series are labelled inside the segments only on the bottom and middle segments of the 1913 column (UK, USA) and the top segment of the 2015 column (China). Values are not printed; estimates: 1913 column: UK about 13.5%, USA about 32%, unlabelled magenta segment about 1% at the top, total about 46%. 2015 column: UK about 2%, USA about 18%, China about 20%, total about 40%. No legend. No highlighted points. The white horizontal baseline runs from about x 7.8% to x 73.8% at y 81.1%.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F (dark slate grey) covering the entire slide. Flat dark slate background #40484F with no gradient or texture. Titles in white (#FFFFFF) ProximaNova-Light (geometric sans-serif, light weight). Subtitle in amber #FCB316, ProximaNova-Light. Chart labels and axis numbers in white #FFFFFF ProximaNova-Regular. Title size about 5.9% of slide height, subtitle about 4.0%, axis labels about 3.0%, segment labels about 2.5%. Chart shapes are flat rectangles with square corners, no outlines, no shadows, no gradients. Baseline is a thin white line about 1 px. Accent colours: amber #FCB316 for subtitle; segment colours orange #D98228, blue #6C96B2, magenta #BE4681.

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a dark slate background. The title sits top-left at about x 3.4%, y 6.1%, with the orange subtitle directly beneath it at y 13.3%. A two-column stacked bar chart occupies the left and centre of the slide, with its baseline at about 81% of the slide height and the right third left empty. The footer line sits bottom-left at about x 2.9%, y 95.5%, and the source line sits bottom-right at about x 74.8%, y 95.5%; both sit on a common baseline with a margin of about 3% from the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart. Title: none printed on the chart itself; slide title 'The spread of computers' with subtitle 'Global installed base'. Y axis: percentage, 0% to 75%, labelled at 0%, 25%, 50%, 75% with no gridlines; the axis runs from 0% at the baseline to 75% at the top. X axis: two categories, 'Computing capacity, 1993' and 'Smartphones, 2018', with no unit. Series: USA (bottom segment, orange #D98228) and Europe (top segment, blue #6C96B2). No values are printed; estimates: Computing capacity, 1993: USA about 49%, Europe about 21%, total about 70%. Smartphones, 2018: USA about 8%, Europe about 14%, total about 22%. Legend: none; series are labelled directly inside the segments of the 2018 column with 'Europe' (blue segment) and 'USA' (orange segment), and not inside the 1993 column. Highlighted points: none. Baseline: a white horizontal axis line from about x 7.8% to 73.8% at y 81%. Small image at x 14.8%, y 95.5%, 1.9% by 3.1%, 57 by 52 pixels, content not legible at this rendering, overlapping the end of the footer line.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F (dark slate grey) covering the entire slide. Flat dark slate background #40484F with no gradient, texture or border. Title in white #FFFFFF, ProximaNova Light (a geometric sans-serif light weight), about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. Subtitle in amber #FCB316, ProximaNova Light, about 4.0% of slide height. Axis labels, category labels and in-bar labels in white #FFFFFF, ProximaNova Regular, about 3.0% of slide height for axes and categories and 2.5% for in-bar labels. Footer and source in small white-grey ProximaNova Regular at about 1.2% of slide height. Chart accents: orange #D98228 for USA, slate blue #6C96B2 for Europe. **Layout**

Single chart slide with a left-aligned title block at top left and the chart filling the middle of the slide. Title and subtitle are left-aligned at about x 3.4%, with the chart's y-axis labels at the left margin and the category labels below the baseline. The footer has the copyright line at bottom left and the source line at bottom right, at about y 95.5%, with no slide number element beyond the 'Page 27' text in the copyright line.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart, two columns, no chart title. Y axis: percentage scale 0% to 75%, labelled at 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, no units beyond %, no gridlines. X axis: two categories, 'Computing capacity, 1993' and 'Smartphones, 2018', baseline drawn as a thin white line. Series: USA in orange, Europe in blue, China in magenta-pink. Column 1 (Computing capacity, 1993), unlabelled segments, about: USA 48.8%, Europe about 20.9%, China about 0.7%, total about 70%. Column 2 (Smartphones, 2018), labelled segments: USA about 8%, Europe about 13%, China about 24%, total about 45%. Legend: none; series are identified by the labels inside column 2. No highlighted points. Column 1 segment boundaries are as measured from the file.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F. Dark slate background #40484F with flat fills and no gradients or shadows. Title in white #FFFFFF, light geometric sans-serif (ProximaNova Light), about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in amber #FCB316, same family light weight, about 3.95% of slide height. Axis and category labels in white #FFFFFF, ProximaNova Regular, about 3.0% of slide height. Segment labels in white #FFFFFF, ProximaNova Regular, about 2.5% of slide height. Series colours: USA orange #D98228, Europe steel blue #6C96B2, China magenta #BE4681. Baseline is a 0.2% thick white line. Columns are flat rectangles with square corners and no outlines. Footer text is small, about 1.5% of slide height, white at reduced opacity.

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a left-aligned title block at top-left (title and orange subtitle stacked, left edge about 3.4% from the slide edge). A stacked column chart occupies the left and centre of the slide, with the y-axis labels at the left and a horizontal baseline below the bars. The footer runs along the bottom: copyright and page number at bottom-left, source line at about 75% across. The right fifth of the slide is empty.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart. Title: none inside the chart; the slide subtitle reads Mobile data traffic (exabytes/month). Y-axis: linear, 0 to 16 in steps of 2, no axis title beyond the subtitle units (exabytes/month). X-axis: two categories, 2014 and 2017. Series, stacked bottom to top: North America (orange #D98228), Western Europe (blue #6C96B2), APAC (magenta #BE4681), All other (grey #838586). Printed values: none; the segments carry only labels, so values are estimates. 2014 column (about 3.7 total): North America about 0.6, Western Europe about 0.4, APAC about 1.3, All other about 1.4. 2017 column (about 14.8 total): North America about 2.5, Western Europe about 1.7, APAC about 7.2, All other about 3.4. Segment labels printed inside the 2017 column: North America, Western Europe, APAC, All other. No legend; series are identified by in-bar labels on the 2017 column only. Highlighted points: none. Baseline: thin white horizontal axis line at 0, spanning from about 7% to 73% of slide width. No gridlines. Columns have square corners and no outlines. Footer and source are text; the two small images near the footer are footer artwork (see elements).

**Design**

Background: Solid charcoal grey #40484F.

Single-column, left-aligned layout with the title and orange subtitle stacked at top left (left margin about 3.4% of width). X axis: categories 2005 and 2016, white baseline. Series (bottom to top, the same colour key for both years): USA, orange #D98228; Western Europe, steel blue #6C96B2; China, magenta-pink #BE4681; All other, mid grey #838586. The 2016 column carries labels USA, Western Europe, China and All other inside its segments; the 2005 column has no segment labels. No values are printed on the slide. Legend: none; segment labels serve as the legend. Highlighted points: none. Bars are flat, unshadowed rectangles, about 13% of slide width each.

### Slide 32

**Review notes**

- Review: Its main technique is progressive build, as in slides 7 to 11 where one stacked bar gains a segment per slide, and slides 32 to 34 where bubbles are added one group at a time.

**Text**

USA in the middle of the pack
Ecommerce share of retail spending, 2017
25%
20%
15%
10%
5%
0%
China
SK
UK
USA
Western
Europe
Japan
India
Eastern
Europe
Russia
Brazil
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 31
Source: Goldman Sachs, ONS

**Layout**

A single chart on a dark slate background. The title sits top-left at x 3.4%, y 6.1%, with the orange subtitle directly beneath it at y 13.3%, both left-aligned to the same margin. The vertical bar chart occupies the left three-quarters of the slide (plot area about x 7.8% to 73.8%, y 25% to 81%), with the y-axis labels to its left and category labels below the baseline. The right quarter of the slide is empty. The footer runs along the bottom at y about 95.5%: copyright text left-aligned at x 3.4%, and the source line left-aligned at x 75%. There is no slide number other than the 'Page 31' printed in the footer.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart. Title: none inside the chart; the slide headline and subtitle serve as the chart heading (subtitle: Ecommerce share of retail spending, 2017). Y-axis: percentage of retail spending, 0% to 25%, gridlines none, tick labels at 0%, 5%, 10%, 15%, 20%, 25%. X-axis: categories, no axis title. Ten bars, one series, with values printed nowhere on the slide; values are estimates: China about 22%, SK about 21%, UK about 17.4%, USA about 9%, Western Europe about 8%, Japan about 6%, India about 5.8%, Eastern Europe about 5.5%, Russia about 5.2%, Brazil about 5.2%. Bars are sorted in descending order, left to right. Highlighted point: the USA bar is orange, all others are steel blue. Legend: none. The baseline is a thin white horizontal line at y about 81%, spanning x about 7.8% to 73.8%. Bar widths are about 2.6% of slide width, evenly spaced at about 6.7% pitch.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat slate #40484F across the full slide. Flat dark slate background with no gradient, texture or imagery. Titles and labels are white (#FFFFFF). The subtitle and no other element uses amber (#FCB316). Bars are steel blue #6C96B2, with the highlighted USA bar in burnt orange #D98228. Typeface is Proxima Nova (a geometric sans-serif): Light for the title and subtitle, Regular for axis and category labels and footer text. Title size is about 5.9% of slide height (cap height about 4.1%), subtitle about 3.95%, axis and category labels about 2.96%, footer text smaller, about 1.5%. ### Slide 33

**Review notes**

- Review: Its main technique is progressive build, as in slides 7 to 11 where one stacked bar gains a segment per slide, and slides 32 to 34 where bubbles are added one group at a time.

**Text**

Global company creation
75%
Bubble size = users
50%
TTM Revenue growth
Facebook
25%
Amazon
Google
0%
0
50
100
150
200
250
Apple
300
TTM Revenue ($bn)
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 32
Source: Bloomberg, companies

**Layout**

A single full-bleed slide with no column grid. The title sits top-left, left-aligned at x 3.4% and y 6.1%. A bubble chart fills the middle and lower area from about x 5% to 76% and y 24% to 90%, with its horizontal axis baseline at y 79%. The note 'Bubble size = users' floats at upper right of the plot at x 61.5% and y 26.2%. A footer strip runs along the bottom at y 95.5%: the copyright line sits at the left margin (x 2.9%) and the source line at x 74.8%. Left margin is about 3%, right margin about 24%. No separate slide number element; the page number 32 is part of the copyright line.

**Visuals**

Bubble chart titled 'Global company creation' with no printed chart title. X-axis: 'TTM Revenue ($bn)', linear, range 0 to 300, ticks labelled every 50 (0, 50, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300). Y-axis: 'TTM Revenue growth', linear, range 0% to 75%, ticks labelled every 25% (0%, 25%, 50%, 75%). Axis units are $bn and percent. The y-axis title is rotated 90 degrees. The x-axis is a white horizontal line only; there is no visible y-axis line and no gridlines. Four bubbles, all filled #6C96B2 with no outline, labelled directly with company names; no printed values, so positions are estimates. Facebook: about 49 $bn TTM revenue and about 46% growth, the second-largest bubble. Google: about 125 $bn and about 25% growth, the largest bubble. Amazon: about 209 $bn and about 38% growth, the smallest bubble. Apple: about 255 $bn and about 14% growth, the third-largest bubble. Bubble size encodes users per the note 'Bubble size = users'; no size legend or scale is printed. No highlighted points and no legend. Logos: none visible on the slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid #40484F dark slate. Dark slate background #40484F. Title, axis labels, tick labels, bubble labels and note are white #FFFFFF. Bubbles are steel blue #6C96B2 with no outline. The x-axis baseline is a thin white #FFFFFF line. Footer text is light grey, approximately #C9D0D6. Typeface is Proxima Nova (geometric sans-serif): Light for the title and the note, Regular for the axis and bubble labels. Title is about 5.9% of slide height. Axis tick labels are about 3.0% and axis title about 3.2%. Bubble labels are about 2.5%. Footer text is about 1.6%. Flat style with no shadows, gradients or corner radii; bubbles are true circles.

**Layout**

Single-column chart slide with the title at top left (x 3.4%, y 6.1%) and a bubble scatter plot filling the middle and lower two-thirds. The y-axis runs up the left side with its title rotated vertically, the x-axis runs along the bottom with its title centred below it, and a note sits at upper right of the plot. The footer is split: copyright and page number at bottom left (x 2.9%), source line at bottom right (x 74.8%), both about 3% of slide height, with left margin about 3.4%.

**Visuals**

Bubble scatter chart. Title: none printed inside the chart area. X axis: 'TTM Revenue ($bn)', linear, labelled 0, 50, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300, range 0 to 300. Y axis: 'TTM Revenue growth', labelled 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, range 0% to about 80%. Series, one per company, bubble size encodes users, all bubbles circular: Facebook centred at about x 50 and y about 47% (bubble diameter about 7.2% of slide width, largest after Google); Google centred at about x 125 and y about 25% (largest bubble, about 8.4% of slide width); Amazon centred at about x 209 and y about 38% (small bubble); Apple centred at about x 255 and y about 14% (medium bubble); Netflix centred at about x 15 and y about 37% (smallest bubble, orange, the only highlighted point). Values are estimates marked 'about' because the slide prints no data labels. Bubbles are labelled by name to their right. Legend: none; a text note reads 'Bubble size = users'. Bubble fill #6C96B2 steel blue except Netflix in orange about #DD8A26. Axis line: single white horizontal baseline at y 0%. No gridlines. Footer contains a small raster copyright line and a small raster source line; a further small raster element sits within the footer at x 14.8%.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F. Dark slate background #40484F with white (#FFFFFF) text throughout. Title in ProximaNova Light, white, about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. Axis tick labels and body text in ProximaNova Regular, white, about 3.0% of slide height; the bubble name labels and the note are about 2.5%; the axis titles are about 3.2%. Accents: bubbles #6C96B2 steel blue, Netflix highlight orange about #DD8A26. Lines: one white baseline about 0.2% of slide height thick. Bubbles have no outline, no shadow, no gradient, and no corner radii. Footer text is small, about 1.5% of slide height, white at reduced opacity.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

### Slide 35

**Text**

Global company creation / 75% / Bubble size = users / Alibaba / 50% / TTM Revenue growth / Tencent / Facebook / 25% / Amazon / Netflix / Baidu / Google / 0% / 0 / 50 / 100 / 150 / 200 / 250 / 300 / TTM Revenue ($bn) / Apple / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 34 / Source: Bloomberg, companies

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a dark grey field. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6% from the top, left-aligned. The bubble chart occupies the centre and lower-left, with the y-axis title rotated vertically at the far left and the x-axis title centred beneath the x-axis. The footer runs along the bottom edge: copyright and page number at bottom-left (x 2.9%), source line at bottom-right (x 74.8%). No slide-level logo.

**Visuals**

Bubble chart with no chart title. X-axis: TTM Revenue ($bn), linear, range 0 to 300, tick labels every 50 (0, 50, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300), white axis baseline. Y-axis: TTM Revenue growth, linear, range 0% to 75%, tick labels 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%; no gridlines. Bubble size encodes users; a legend-style note reads 'Bubble size = users' with no size scale. Bubbles with about values: Alibaba about x 43 $bn, about y 67%, pink; Tencent about x 43 $bn, about y 54%, pink, overlapping Facebook; Facebook about x 49 $bn, about y 46%, blue, largest on the left cluster and partly behind Tencent; Netflix about x 15 $bn, about y 37%, small orange bubble overlapping Baidu's top edge; Baidu about x 15 $bn, about y 32%, pink; Amazon about x 209 $bn, about y 38%, small blue; Google about x 125 $bn, about y 25%, large blue, the largest bubble on the chart; Apple about x 255 $bn, about y 14%, blue, the second-largest bubble. Each bubble has its name as a label immediately to its right. No highlighted points or legend colour key.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F dark slate grey. Flat dark slate background, no gradients or textures. Title in white (#FFFFFF) light geometric sans-serif (ProximaNova-Light), about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. Axis tick labels and axis titles in white (#FFFFFF) ProximaNova-Regular, about 3.0% of slide height for ticks and 3.2% for axis titles; bubble labels about 2.5%. Bubble fills: pink #BE4681 for Alibaba, Tencent and Baidu; steel blue #6C96B2 for Facebook, Amazon, Google and Apple; orange about #D9822B for Netflix. Axis baseline a thin white line about 0.1% stroke. Bubbles are flat circles with no outline or shadow. Footer text is small white ProximaNova-Regular at about 1.4% of slide height.

### Slide 36

**Text**

Unicorns are born outside Silicon Valley! / Last reported valuation ($bn) / 80 / 60 / 40 / 20 / 0 / Byte / Meituan / Didi / Lufax / Spotify / Flipcart / DJI / Grab / Ola / dance Dianping Chuxing / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 35 / Source: Press reports, companies, Bloomberg

**Layout**

Single vertical bar chart occupying the left and centre of the slide, from about x 3% to x 74%; the right quarter is empty. Title is top-left at x 3.4%, y 6.1%, with the amber subtitle directly beneath it at x 3.5%, y 13.3%, both left-aligned on a left margin of about 3.5%. Footer copyright line sits bottom-left at y 95.5% and the source line sits bottom-right, ending at about x 91%, both on the same baseline.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart, title 'Unicorns are born outside Silicon Valley!' with subtitle 'Last reported valuation ($bn)'. Y-axis labelled in $bn with ticks 0, 20, 40, 60, 80 (range 0 to 80), no gridlines. X-axis is categorical with nine companies. Single series, bars in #6C96B2 steel blue, no data labels printed. Values are not printed, so estimates are: ByteDance about 75, Meituan Dianping about 55, Didi Chuxing about 50, Lufax about 35, Spotify about 25, Flipcart about 21, DJI about 15, Grab about 10, Ola about 4. Bars are in descending order from left to right, evenly spaced, each about 3% of slide width wide. Horizontal baseline axis line in light grey #D9D9D9 at y about 80.9%, spanning x about 7.2% to 73.2%, about 2px thick. No legend. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate grey #40484F. Flat dark slate background with no gradient, texture or imagery. Title in white #FFFFFF ProximaNova Light (geometric humanist sans-serif, light weight), about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. Subtitle in amber #FCB316 ProximaNova Light, about 4.0% of slide height. Axis tick labels and category labels in white #FFFFFF ProximaNova Regular, about 3.0% of slide height. Footer and source text in small light grey-white ProximaNova Regular, about 1.5% of slide height. Bars are flat rectangles in #6C96B2, square corners, no shadows, no outlines. Axis line is a thin light grey rule.

©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 36
Source: CAE, Pitchbook

**Layout**

Single chart slide with a left-aligned title block at the top (title at about 6% from the top, subtitle beneath it) and a stacked column chart spanning the lower two-thirds. The left margin is about 3.4% of the slide width. The chart's y-axis labels sit at the left, the two columns sit in the centre-left, and the x-axis baseline runs to about 74% of the width. The footer copyright line sits bottom left at about 96% of the height, with the source line bottom right at about 75% of the width. No slide number appears apart from 'Page 36' in the footer.

**Visuals**

Chart: 100% stacked column chart with no chart title inside the plot. Y-axis runs 0% to 100% in 25% steps, labelled 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, 100%, with no unit beyond the percent sign. X-axis categories are 1995 and 2017, with a thin white horizontal baseline at 0%. Series, bottom to top: USA (blue #6C96B2), China (orange #D98228), India (magenta #BE4681), UK (green #95CA53). Values are not printed; estimates: 1995 column has USA about 95%, China about 1%, India about 1%, UK about 2%, total about 99%. 2017 column has USA about 51%, China about 24%, India about 4%, UK about 4%, total about 84%. Segment labels are printed inside the 2017 column only: UK (top), India, China, USA. No legend. No highlighted points. Column width is equal for both bars.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F. Dark slate background #40484F with no gradient or texture. Title text in Proxima Nova Light, white #FFFFFF, about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. Subtitle in Proxima Nova Light, amber #FCB316, about 4.0% of slide height. Axis and category labels in Proxima Nova Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 3.0% of slide height. Footer and source text is small light grey-white #C8CDD2, about 1.3% of slide height, in a light sans-serif. Series fills are flat with no outlines, no shadows, and no corner radii. Baseline is a 1px white line.

**Layout**

Single left-aligned column with a title at top-left and a subtitle beneath it. A simple two-bar column chart sits in the left portion of the slide, from about 2% to 42% of width, and the right half is empty. The footer runs along the bottom with the copyright and page number at bottom-left and the source line at bottom-centre-right, both about 3% above the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Column chart, titled 'Market size, 2017 ($tr)'. Y-axis: linear, labelled with integers 0 to 6 with no unit label on the axis itself (unit given in subtitle as $tr, trillions of dollars), range 0 to 6, no gridlines. X-axis: two categories, 'US ecommerce' and 'US retail', with a thin white horizontal baseline at 0. Series (one series, two bars, no printed data values): US ecommerce, orange bar, about 0.45 ($tr); US retail, blue-grey bar, about 5.75 ($tr). Values are estimates read from bar heights against the axis scale. No legend. No highlighted points. Bars are flat rectangles with no outline. Bar width about 6.6% of slide width.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat slate grey #40484F. Flat dark slate background, no gradient or texture. Title and body labels in white, subtitle in amber yellow. Typeface is Proxima Nova (a geometric humanist sans-serif): title in Light weight, labels in Regular weight. Title cap height is about 5.9% of slide height; subtitle about 4.0%; axis and category labels about 3.0%. Bars are flat fills with square corners and no shadow; the baseline is a thin white line. Footer and source text are small, light grey, Regular weight.

Retail: no printed value, about 25 (estimate). Consumer spending: $40tr (printed label above bar, about 40 on the axis). Bars are steel blue, except the US ecommerce bar which is orange. No legend. Highlighted points: the orange US ecommerce bar and the orange subtitle text. Baseline is a thin white horizontal line at 0. Footer logos/images: none beyond the footer text rendered as images.

Axis tick labels and category labels in white ProximaNova Regular, about 3.0% of slide height. Value callouts in white ProximaNova Light, about 2.5% of slide height. Bars are flat steel blue #6C96B2 with no outline, no shadows, square corners; the highlighted bar is orange. Baseline is a thin white rule. Footer text small, white-grey, about 1.5% of slide height.

Page 40

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark slate slide split into a title band, a two-column comparison and a footer strip. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% left margin and 6% from the top. Two amber column headings sit left-aligned at about 23% from the top, one over each column, with the right column starting at about 53.5%. Below them a split band from about 30% to 89% of the height holds a darker left panel and a lighter right panel divided by a dark chevron. Body items are left-aligned in each column with a 3.9% (left) and 53.9% (right) inset. The footer copyright line sits bottom-left at about 3% from the left edge and 95.5% from the top. There is no slide number element beyond the printed 'Page 40' in the footer.

**Visuals**

Two-tone comparison panel with a chevron divider. There is no photograph, chart, table or logo. The left panel is a mid-dark grey rectangle (#626465) from x 0 to about 48.8% and y 30.1% to 89.4%. The right panel is a lighter grey rectangle (#838586) from about x 39.6% to the right edge and the same vertical span. Between them a dark slate chevron band (#40484F) about 2.9 to 4% of the width wide points right; its top starts near x 42.3% and its point is near x 48.8% at about 60% of the height, before returning to the bottom near x 43.9%. The chevron visually separates the two panels. Nothing else is drawn.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F across the whole slide. Flat, minimal, no gradients, shadows or rounded corners. Background is dark slate #40484F. Title is white #FFFFFF in Proxima Nova Light (sans-serif), about 5.9% of slide height. Column headings are amber #FCB316 in Proxima Nova Regular, about 4.4% of slide height. Body text is white #FFFFFF in Proxima Nova Regular, about 4.0% of slide height, with generous spacing between items. Left panel #626465, right panel #838586, chevron band #40484F. The footer is a small light grey sans-serif line, about 1.3% of slide height.

**Layout**

Centre: a solid flat gray (#7F7F7F) right-pointing block arrow with a rectangular shaft and triangular head, no outline, at x 36.6%, y 37.7%, w 15.6%, h 6.7%. Right: DoorDash logo, a red-orange (#FF3008) stylised D-shaped chevron icon above the uppercase wordmark 'DOORDASH' in a wide geometric sans-serif, occupying about x 59.4%, y 29.7%, w 26.3%, h 22.7%. The arrow and footer accent are neutral grays (#7F7F7F and a light gray around #C8CCD0). Typeface is Proxima Nova (geometric humanist sans-serif): title in Light weight, bullets in Regular weight. Title height is about 5.9% of slide height (cap height about 4.1%); body text is about 3.5% of slide height. Shapes are flat with no corner radii, shadows or outlines.

A single stacked column chart with no axes, gridlines or legend. ### Slide 45

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A simple two-column comparison with a short label per side makes a conceptual distinction readable at a glance.
- What would change the score: I would merge some of the repeated build steps (for example slides 44 to 47 where the same two empty circles recur) into fewer slides, and add an agenda or progress marker so the 106-page route is easier to follow.

**Text**

Two models of retail
Packets versus preference
Retail as logistics
Sears, Walmart
Retail as tastemaker
Boutiques, big cities
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 44

**Layout**

Left-aligned title block at top-left (x 3.4%, y 6.1%) with the subtitle directly beneath it. Two equal-sized circles sit side by side in the lower-middle band, the left circle centred near x 25% and the right near x 75%, with a double-headed arrow bridging the gap between them. A small copyright footer sits bottom-left at about y 95.5%, with no separate slide number element.

**Visuals**

Two outlined circles and one double-headed connector. Left circle: outline ring in orange (about #D98025), stroke about 0.4% of slide width, no fill, diameter about 36.4% of slide width, top-left corner at x 7.0%, y 24.6%, containing the bold heading 'Retail as logistics' and the line 'Sears, Walmart'. Right circle: outline ring in steel blue (about #6A96B0), same stroke width and size, top-left corner at x 56.6%, y 24.6%, containing 'Retail as tastemaker' and 'Boutiques, big cities'. Connector: a horizontal dotted line in light grey (about #A8A8A8), roughly 1% of slide height thick, from x 43.4% to x 56.6% at y about 56.6%, with solid filled triangular arrowheads at both ends, the left pointing left and the right pointing right. The connector touches the outer edges of both circles at the vertical centre line of the circles. Two small raster images sit in the footer: one about 194 by 26 px (x 2.9%, y 95.5%) carrying the copyright text in light grey-white, and one about 56 by 52 px (x 14.8%, y 95.5%) whose content is not legible at this size. No photographs, charts, or icons.

**Design**

Background: Flat solid charcoal #40484F. Dark charcoal slide with a flat background of #40484F. Title 'Two models of retail' in ProximaNova Light, white #FFFFFF, about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. Subtitle 'Packets versus preference' in ProximaNova Light, gold/amber #FCB316, about 4.0% of slide height. Circle headings in ProximaNova Bold, white, about 4.4% of slide height. Circle descriptors in ProximaNova Regular, white, about 3.5% of slide height. Footer text is very small, light grey-white, ProximaNova Regular. Shapes are flat with no shadows, gradients, or corner radii (circles are true circles). Margins about 3.4% left.

### Slide 46

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: I would merge some of the repeated build steps (for example slides 44 to 47 where the same two empty circles recur) into fewer slides, and add an agenda or progress marker so the 106-page route is easier to follow.

**Text**

So far, the internet has mostly tackled logistics
Packets versus preference
Retail as logistics
Sears, Walmart
Amazon
Retail as tastemaker
Boutiques, big cities
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 45

**Layout**

Left-aligned title block across the top with the title at about 6% from the top and the subtitle beneath it, starting at the left margin of about 3.4%. Two equal outlined circles sit side by side in the lower two-thirds, centred at roughly 25% and 75% of the width, joined by a double-headed dotted arrow between them. Each circle carries a centred two- or three-line label. The copyright and page footer sits bottom-left at about 95.5% from the top, with no slide number element separate from it.

**Visuals**

Two outlined circles and one double-headed connector arrow; no photographs, charts or logos. Left circle: outline ring in burnt orange #D9822B, stroke about 0.4% of slide width, unfilled, diameter about 36% of slide width, containing the bold heading 'Retail as logistics' and the lines 'Sears, Walmart' and bold 'Amazon'. Right circle: outline ring in steel blue #5E8FAE, same stroke weight and size, unfilled, containing the bold heading 'Retail as tastemaker' and 'Boutiques, big cities'. Connector: a horizontal double-headed arrow between the circles at about 56% from the top, spanning the gap from the left circle's right edge to the right circle's left edge, dotted line in mid grey #A6A6A6 with solid filled triangular arrowheads of the same grey at both ends.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F (dark slate grey). Flat dark slate background #40484F with no gradient, texture or imagery. Titles and body text are white #FFFFFF. The subtitle and accent colour is amber #FCB316. Typeface is Proxima Nova (geometric humanist sans-serif): the title and subtitle are Light, the circle headings are Bold, the circle body lines are Regular, and 'Amazon' is Bold. The title is about 5.9% of slide height and the subtitle about 4.0%; the circle headings are about 4.4% and the body lines about 3.5%. Circle outlines are thin, unfilled, with no shadow, corner radius or rotation. Arrow is dotted with solid triangular heads, no shadow.

**Text**

And pushing into new kinds of decision
Packets versus preference

Retail as tastemaker
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 47

**Layout**

Single row of three elements centred vertically in the lower two-thirds: a left-aligned title and subtitle sit in the top-left corner at about 6% from the top and 3.4% from the left margin. Two equal outlined circles flank a grey right-pointing triangle, with the circle labels centred in each circle. The copyright and page line sits bottom-left at about 95.5% from the top, with no slide number element beyond the footer text.

**Visuals**

Two circle outlines and one triangle, no photographs or charts. Left circle: outline ring, orange (#D7802A), about 2 px stroke, unfilled, centred near x 25%, y 63%, roughly 36% of slide width and 65% of slide height in diameter. Right circle: outline ring, steel blue (#5E93B0), same stroke weight and size, centred near x 75%, y 63%. Between them: a solid right-pointing isosceles triangle with fill #7F7F7F, apex pointing right at about x 53.5%, y 53.5%, base vertical on the left edge at x 46.5%, spanning y 26.9% to 86.5%. No shadows, no gradients. The footer line is a small raster image of the copyright text, about 194 by 26 px, with a second small 55 by 52 px image at x 14.8% overlapping the footer text area.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F dark slate grey, with a #FFFFFF base rectangle beneath it that is fully covered. Flat dark slate background with no texture. Title white (#FFFFFF) Proxima Nova Light, regular weight, sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height in size with normal case. Subtitle in amber (#FCB316) Proxima Nova Light, about 3.95% of slide height. Circle labels white (#FFFFFF) Proxima Nova Bold, about 4.44% of slide height. Footer text small, light grey-white, about 1% of slide height. Line style: thin uniform ring strokes, no corner radii needed as shapes are circles and a sharp triangle. No drop shadows.
