### Slide 49

**Text**

(All of which will affect the USA more than most…) / Retail square feet per capita, 2017 / 25 / 20 / 15 / 10 / 5 / 0 / USA / Canada / Australia / UK / France / China / Germany / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 48 / Source: iCSC

**Layout**

Single chart slide with no grid or columns. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top and the subtitle sits directly beneath it, both left-aligned to a left margin of about 3.4%. A vertical bar chart occupies the left-centre area from about 7% to 73% horizontally and 24% to 90% vertically, with the y-axis labels at the left and the category labels beneath the baseline. The right quarter of the slide is empty. The footer copyright sits bottom-left at about 95.5% from the top, and the source line sits bottom-right of the chart area at about 75% horizontally. There is no visible slide-number field beyond the 'Page 48' text in the footer.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart, untitled inside the chart (the title and subtitle sit above it as slide text). Y-axis: unlabelled units, scale 0 to 25 with gridlines absent and tick labels at 0, 5, 10, 15, 20 and 25. X-axis: seven categories in this order, USA, Canada, Australia, UK, France, China, Germany. Series: one series, retail square feet per capita, 2017. Values: USA 24 (the slide prints no value; about 24), Canada about 16, Australia about 11, UK about 5, France about 4, China about 3, Germany about 2. Highlighted point: the USA bar is orange #D98228 while the other six bars are steel blue #6C96B2. Bars are flat rectangles of equal width (about 3.8% of slide width) with even spacing. The baseline is a thin white horizontal axis line at the zero value running from about 7.2% to 73.2% of slide width. No legend. The two small raster images at the footer positions (194 by 26 px at the bottom-left and 82 by 27 px at the bottom-right, and a 56 by 52 px image near 14.8%) appear to carry the footer and source text; their content is not separately legible.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat slate grey #40484F covering the full slide. Flat dark slate background with no gradient, texture or photograph. Title text is white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Light (geometric sans-serif, light weight), sized about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle is amber #FCB316 in ProximaNova Light at about 4.0% of slide height. Axis tick labels and category labels are white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Regular (sans-serif, regular weight) at about 3.0% of slide height. Footer and source text are small, light grey-white, about 1.2% of slide height, in a light sans-serif. Accent colour is orange #D98228 on the USA bar. Bars have square corners, no shadows, no outlines. Axis line is a thin white stroke about 0.2% of height. Margins are roughly 3.4% left and 6% top.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#40484F]: Full-slide background rectangle, solid fill (Flat fill, no border, no shadow, covers entire slide. A white fill rectangle of the same extent is also present underneath, fully covered.)

### Slide 50

**Text**

“Ecommerce is asset-light”

**Layout**

Single-title slide: a dark slate header band runs across the top of the slide with the title left-aligned at about 3.4% from the left edge and vertically centred in the band. The photograph fills the slide behind the band and runs full-bleed to the left, right and bottom edges. No footer, page number or other text is visible.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph of three parked cream-coloured delivery vans (webvan.com trucks) photographed head-on in a row, slightly off-centre, with the front of the nearest truck at lower left and the other two receding to the right. Each cab carries a blue and green stylised 'W' mark on the roof and side, the blue 'webvan.com' wordmark on the front bumper area, and 'USDOT 898472' lettering on the doors and lower body. The windscreens are dark navy and show steering wheels and seats; amber marker lights sit on the cab roofs and the front corners. Two white licence plates read '39 331 F' and '37 817 F'. Lighting is flat daylight, the composition is a straight-on horizontal row cropped at the bottom edge, and the dominant colours are cream/beige, navy and blue, with silver bumpers. The photo is not tinted; the only overlay is the slate band across its top. A small image of 207 by 26 pixels sits at the bottom left (x 2.9%, y 95.5%, w 13.8%, h 3.1%); its content is not clearly distinguishable in the photograph at that spot and it may be a small logo.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed photograph with a flat slate header band across the top. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) in a light geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova Light), not bold, about 5.9% of slide height with cap height about 4.2% of slide height, in sentence case with no letter-spacing changes. The header band is slate grey #40484F, a sharp-cornered rectangle with no shadow or gradient, running the full width and about 21.8% of slide height. There are no body text or accent colours; the only accent colours come from the photograph (blue #2A5BD7-like and green #7BC043-like logo marks).

### Slide 52

**Text**

“Ecommerce is asset-light”

**Layout**

A full-width dark grey title band runs across the top 11% of the slide, with a single left-aligned title near the vertical centre of the band at about 3.4% from the left edge. Below the band, a full-bleed aerial photograph fills the rest of the slide with no margin, footer or slide number visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph, full-bleed below the title band and cropped at the left, right and bottom edges. Subject: an aerial view of a large gravel vehicle yard holding rows of dark navy-blue high-roof Mercedes-Benz Sprinter vans, parked nose-on in the upper half of the frame. In the lower half, two larger vans sit side-on and angled toward the viewer, each with white 'prime' lettering and a light-blue Amazon smile-arrow logo on the side panel. Lighting is flat, even daylight with soft shadows. Dominant colours are navy blue (about #2B3A4E), pale grey gravel (about #D9DCDD) and light-blue logo accents (about #1EA1E0). A few green grass tufts show along the bottom edge. A small placed image (207 by 26 pixels) sits at the lower left of the slide over the photograph, and its content is not distinguishable from the photograph at that spot.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F band across the top 11% of the slide, with the full-bleed photograph occupying the remaining area below it. Dark slate title band #40484F covering the top 11% of the slide, with no border or shadow. Title text is white #FFFFFF in Proxima Nova Light, sans-serif, regular weight, about 5.9% of slide height (cap height about 4.2% of slide height), with no capitals styling. No body text, accent colour or decorative shapes are present. Corners are square and there are no gradients or shadows.

### Slide 53

**Text**

New problems: groceries / US market size, 2017 ($tr) / 1.5 / 1.0 / 0.5 / 0.0 / Ecommerce / Groceries / Groceries breaks the Amazon model, / and the whole ecommerce mode / Can’t be bolted onto a commodity / logistics platform / Needs a whole new platform / But so big it’s worth it / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 52 / Source: BLS

**Layout**

Single-slide layout with a left chart panel and a right text column. The title sits top-left at about x 3.4%, y 6.1%, with the orange subtitle directly below it at y 13.3%. The chart occupies the left half (about x 3% to 41%, y 24% to 96%), with the value axis labels stacked at the far left and the two category labels under the baseline. The right text block starts at x 51.7% and runs down from y 32% to 79%, left-aligned with a single-line gap between paragraphs. The footer runs along the bottom edge: copyright line at bottom-left (x 2.9%) and source line at bottom-right area (x 74.8%), both at y 95.5%. No slide number is printed as a separate element; the page number 52 is part of the footer copyright line.

**Visuals**

Bar chart, vertical bars, no chart title inside the plot (the slide title and subtitle serve as the title). Value axis on the left with labelled ticks 0.0, 0.5, 1.0 and 1.5, units $tr (stated in the subtitle as US market size, 2017 ($tr)), range 0.0 to 1.5. Category axis with no axis title; horizontal white baseline at 0.0 running from about x 7% to x 41%. Two categories with labels: Ecommerce and Groceries. Series: one series with two bars. Ecommerce bar is steel blue #6C96B2, about 0.45 (no value printed on slide). Groceries bar is orange #D98228, about 1.3 (no value printed on slide). No legend. No highlighted points; the orange bar is the visually emphasised one. Small logo-like images are present only in the footer band: a 194 by 26 px footer copyright text image at left, a 76 by 27 px image at x 74.8% reading Source: BLS, and a 57 by 52 px image at x 14.8% whose content is not legible in the render.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat fill #40484F (dark slate grey). Flat dark slate background #40484F with no gradient, texture or image. Titles and body text are white #FFFFFF. Subtitle and accent are amber #FCB316. Chart bars are steel blue #6C96B2 and burnt orange #D98228; axis baseline and tick labels white. Typefaces are Proxima Nova Light for the title and subtitle and Proxima Nova Regular for body, chart labels and footer, all geometric sans-serif. Title height about 5.9% of slide height at Light weight; body text about 4.4% at Regular; axis and category labels about 3.0%; footer text is very small. Lines are a thin white baseline with no shadows, no rounded corners, no shadows and no gradients. Bars are square-cornered rectangles with no outline.

### Slide 56

**Text**

More interesting: changing groceries / Items stocked (000) / 140 / 120 / 100 / 80 / 60 / 40 / 20 / 0 / Supermarket, / 1975 / Supermarket, / 2018 / Wal-mart / 'supercenter' / Different buying journeys mean / different baskets / Different brands, SKUs and SKU counts / Shift from advertising to marketing? / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 55 / Source: a16z, Walmart

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a dark slate background: a bar chart occupies the left half (about x 2 to 46 percent) and three statements sit in a left-aligned column at about x 51.7 percent on the right. The title is top-left at about x 3.4, y 6.1 with a yellow subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on a common left margin of about 3.4 percent. The footer runs along the bottom edge at about y 95.5 with the copyright and page number at the left margin and the source line at about x 74.8 on the right.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart with three bars in steel blue #6C96B2, no chart title inside the chart area; the subtitle 'Items stocked (000)' serves as the axis unit. Y-axis runs from 0 to 140 in steps of 20 (labels 0, 20, 40, 60, 80, 100, 120, 140), unlabelled axis title, values in thousands of items. X-axis is a single white horizontal baseline at 0 with three categories: 'Supermarket, 1975', 'Supermarket, 2018' and 'Wal-mart 'supercenter''. Printed values: none on the bars. Estimated values (about): Supermarket 1975 about 8, Supermarket 2018 about 30, Wal-mart supercenter about 120. No legend and no highlighted points. Bars are flat rectangles about 4 percent of slide width each, evenly spaced, with the 1975 bar at about x 11.5, the 2018 bar at about x 24.5 and the Wal-mart bar at about x 37.2. The measured fill box at x 11.5, y 33.1, w 29.7, h 44.4 spans the bar group from the 1975 bar to the Wal-mart bar. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid slate colour #40484F covering the whole slide. Flat dark slate background with no gradient or texture. Title and body text are white #FFFFFF, the subtitle is amber #FCB316, bars and accents are steel blue #6C96B2 and the axis line is white. Typeface is Proxima Nova (geometric humanist sans-serif): Light for title and subtitle, Regular for axis labels and body. Title is about 5.9 percent of slide height, subtitle about 4.0 percent, body statements about 4.4 percent, axis and category labels about 3.0 percent, footer about 1.3 percent. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows, and the chart has no gridlines.

Shapes are flat with no corner radius, shadow or gradient.

### Slide 60

**Text**

Things that shouldn’t work
Fashion
“Online? Really?”
Warby Parker
“Glasses?
Showrooms?!”
Cosmetics
“Makeup you
haven’t tried?!!”
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 59

**Layout**

Three equal outlined circles sit in one horizontal row across the middle-lower area of the slide, centred at about 15%, 49% and 83% of the slide width, with their tops at about 33% and bottoms at about 78% of the height. Each circle holds a bold heading and a regular quotation, centred. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge, aligned to the left margin, with no subtitle. A small copyright and page line sits bottom-left at about 3% from the left edge and 96% from the top. No slide-number box is present beyond the footer text.

**Visuals**

Three outlined circles, no fill, drawn as thin strokes of about 0.35% of slide height. Left circle: orange outline (about #D98324), centred near x 15.4%, y 55.6%, diameter about 25% of slide width. Middle circle: steel-blue outline (about #6B9BB5), centred near x 49.4%, y 55.6%, same size. Right circle: magenta-pink outline (about #C7457F), centred near x 83.3%, y 55.6%, same size. Each circle contains text only (heading and quotation); no icons, photographs or charts. A small raster image (57 by 52 px) sits at the footer near x 14.8%, y 95.5%, overlapping the footer text area; it shows no distinct visible content.

**Design**

**Layout**

Single-slide chart layout on a full-bleed dark background. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6% from the top, with the subtitle directly beneath it in amber. A single vertical bar chart fills the middle and lower area, with its baseline at about 79.5% of the height and its x-axis labels beneath it. The legend sits top-right of the plot at about 76% from the left. The footer line sits bottom-left at about 96% of the height, and the source line sits bottom-right at about 75%. Left alignment is used for the title, subtitle and y-axis labels.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical stacked column chart, quarterly unit deliveries in thousands (000). There is no printed chart title beyond the subtitle 'Quarterly unit deliveries (000)'. Y-axis is labelled 0, 30, 60, 90 (units: thousand units), no gridlines. X-axis shows labels Jun-15, Dec-15, Jun-16, Dec-16, Jun-17, Dec-17, Jun-18 at every second column, which is quarterly data from Jun-15 to Sep-18 (14 columns). Series 1, Models S & X (blue #6C96B2, bottom segment): no values printed; about 11, 12, 17, 15, 15, 25, 22, 25, 22, 26, 28, 22, 22, 28 (thousands, estimated from bar heights). Series 2, Model 3 (orange #D98228, top segment): no values printed; about 0 for the first nine columns, about 1 at column 10, about 2 at column 11, about 8 at column 12, about 19 at column 13, about 56 at column 14, so totals about 27, 30, 30, 41 and 84 at the last four columns. Legend at top right: orange square 'Model 3', blue square 'Models S & X'. No highlighted points. Source line: 'Source: Tesla'. Three placed images sit in the footer band (see elements); they are not legible in the render.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat fill, dark slate #40484F. Dark slate background #40484F with a flat fill and no gradient or texture. Title text is white #FFFFFF in a light geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova Light), roughly 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle is amber #FCB316 in the same light sans, roughly 4% of slide height. Axis labels and legend text are white #FFFFFF in Proxima Nova Regular, roughly 3% of slide height. Footer and source text are small white sans, about 1.5% of slide height, slightly muted. Bars are flat fills with no outlines or shadows: blue #6C96B2 and orange #D98228. The x-axis is a thin white horizontal rule. No rounded corners, shadows or gradients.

Source: Autonews, Tesla

**Layout**

Single full-bleed slide with a left-aligned title block at top left (title and orange subtitle stacked, left edge about 3.4% from the slide edge). A vertical-bar chart sits in the left two-thirds, with its baseline ending at about 64% of the slide width, leaving the right third empty. The footer runs along the bottom edge: copyright and page number at bottom left, source line at bottom right, both about 4% from the bottom and about 3% from the side margins.

**Visuals**

Bar chart, no chart title beyond the subtitle 'Q3 2018 US car sales (000)'. Vertical bars on a dark background. Y-axis: unlabelled units, numeric scale 0 to 100 with tick labels 0, 25, 50, 75, 100 (ticks spaced evenly, 0 at the baseline). X-axis: categories Tesla, Lexus, Mercedes, BMW, Audi, Lincoln, left to right, with a thin white horizontal baseline at 0. Series (one series, values are estimates marked 'about' because the slide prints no bar values): Tesla about 84 (highlighted in orange); Lexus about 79; Mercedes about 78; BMW about 72; Audi about 60; Lincoln about 25. Legend: none. Highlighted point: the Tesla bar, orange #D98228; all other bars #6C96B2 steel blue. Bars are flat rectangles, equal width about 3.8% of slide width, spaced evenly. Small image fragment (56 by 52 px) at about x 14.8%, y 95.5% overlapping the footer copyright line.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F (dark slate grey). Flat dark slate background #40484F with no gradient, texture or shadow. Title text white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Light (geometric humanist sans-serif, light weight, regular case), about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in amber #FCB316, ProximaNova Light, about 4.0% of slide height. Axis tick labels and category labels white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Regular, about 3.0% of slide height. Footer and source text in light grey-white, ProximaNova Regular, about 1.1% of slide height. Accent colours: orange #D98228 for the highlighted Tesla bar; steel blue #6C96B2 for the other bars. Bars have square corners and no outline. Baseline is a thin white line about 0.1% of height. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 67

**Text**

More interesting: software and silicon eat cars / Component cost share / 100% / 75% / Tier 1s / 50% / OEM / Raw materials / 25% / Powertrain / (LG) / 0% / Chevy Bolt / Forecast 2025 battery cost / Generic ICE car / decline / Battery (LG) / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 66 / Source: UBS

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a dark grey field. The title sits top-left at about 6% of slide height with an amber subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on a left margin of about 3.5%. A stacked column chart spans the middle of the slide with three columns; the y-axis labels run down the left side and the category labels sit under the baseline. A footer line at the bottom left carries the copyright and page number and a source note sits at bottom right near x 75%. The right fifth of the slide is empty.

**Visuals**

Chart: 100% stacked column chart, no chart title beyond the slide subtitle 'Component cost share'. Y-axis runs 0% to 100% in 25% steps, labelled 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, 100%, with no axis title. X-axis is categorical with three categories: 'Chevy Bolt', 'Forecast 2025 battery cost decline', 'Generic ICE car'. No values are printed on the segments; the segment shares below are estimates marked about. Chevy Bolt column (bottom to top): Battery (LG) about 44%, Powertrain (LG) about 13%, dark slate blue segment about 6%, mid blue about 10%, light blue Tier 1s segment about 28%; no segment labels are shown on this column. Forecast 2025 battery cost decline column (bottom to top): Battery (LG) about 26%, Powertrain (LG) about 17%, Raw materials about 7%, OEM about 14%, Tier 1s about 36%; each segment is labelled in white text inside the segment. Generic ICE car column (bottom to top): unlabelled dark slate blue segment about 10%, mid blue about 20%, light blue about 70%. No legend. No highlighted points. Segment colours: Battery orange #D98228, Powertrain light orange #E8B47E, dark slate #4A728D, mid blue #6C96B2, light blue #A7C0D1. Baseline is a thin white horizontal axis line at 0% running from x about 10% to x about 74%. Logos and icons: none. Small raster images at footer: a 194 by 26 pixel copyright text image bottom left, a 79 by 27 pixel source text image bottom right, and a 57 by 52 pixel small image at x 14.8% with no visible content.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat dark slate grey #40484F. Flat dark slate background #40484F with no gradient or texture. Title in white #FFFFFF ProximaNova Light, sans-serif, regular weight, about 5.9% of slide height for cap-height-including line, sentence case. Subtitle in amber #FCB316 ProximaNova Light about 4% of slide height. Axis and category labels in white #FFFFFF ProximaNova Regular about 3% of slide height for the y-axis and category labels and about 2.5% for segment labels. Footer and source text are tiny, around 1.2% of slide height, light grey-white. Bars are flat solid fills with no outlines, no corner radius, no shadows and no gradients; segments are stacked with no gaps between them. Baseline is a thin white rule.

### Slide 68

**Text**

More interesting: changing what cars are
Software and batteries are unbundling cars and car journeys
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 67

**Layout**

Left-aligned title block in the top-left, with the title at about 6% from the top and the subtitle directly beneath it, both starting at about 3.4% from the left margin. Three equal-size circular photographs sit in a horizontal row across the vertical middle of the slide, spaced evenly, with the first photo at the left margin and the third ending near the right side. The copyright footer sits bottom-left at about 95.5% from the top and is the only footer element. No slide number field is present beyond the printed 'Page 67' in the footer.

**Visuals**

Three circular photographs in a row, each cropped to a circle with a thin steel-blue outline.
1) Left: a driverless shuttle, the 'olli' autonomous minibus, white and black body with teal horizontal accent stripes and 'olli' lettering on the side panels. It is shown in three-quarter front view, facing left, with a large glass-walled cabin and a roof sensor box. The background is a dusk sky grading from blue at the top to orange at the horizon, with a white Ferris wheel and water at the right edge. Lighting is low, warm sunset light; the bus is dark grey at the wheels. The circle is cropped tightly, with the bus filling most of the frame.
2) Centre: an electric kick scooter, the 'Lime-S', with a green stem cover bearing 'Lime-S' lettering, black folding stem, white deck with 'LIME S' printed on the deck, black handlebar and a black front wheel. It is shown diagonally on a plain white background, lit evenly with no shadow emphasis. The scooter is cropped by the circle at the lower left corner, so the deck edge runs off the circle.
3) Right: a green articulated tram-like vehicle with a glossy bright green body, grey roof-mounted equipment, and a large red ribbon or bow tied to the front bumper. Two people are visible in the front windscreen cab. It is seen in a three-quarter front view, facing towards the viewer, on a dark grey road with white dashed centre markings. The background is a red-brown dirt bank with green grass and a green metal guardrail at the left. Lighting is daylight, mostly even, with a slight gloss on the bodywork. The circle crop shows the whole front and side of the vehicle.

**Design**

Background: Solid slate grey #40484F covering the full slide. Flat dark slate background with no gradient, pattern or texture. The title is in a light-weight geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova Light or closest equivalent), white #FFFFFF, at about 5.9% of slide height. The subtitle is the same family and weight in amber #FCB316 at about 4.0% of slide height. The footer is a small sans-serif in light grey-white at about 1.2% of slide height. The circular photos have a thin steel-blue outline about #6A9BC3, roughly 0.3% of slide height wide, with no shadow, no corner radius needed (they are circles), and no overlay or tint on the photographs.

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a full-bleed dark background. Title is top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6% from the top, with a yellow subtitle directly beneath it. The footer runs along the bottom at about 95.5% from the top: copyright and page number at the bottom left, source line at the bottom right, both small and left-aligned.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart of non-sports programming spend, 2017, in $bn. Title: none inside the chart; slide title and subtitle sit above it. Y-axis: 0 to 12, tick labels at 0, 2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12 in white, no axis line, no gridlines. X-axis: categories NBCU, Fox, TW, Disney, Netflix, Viacom, Amazon, CBS, labels in white beneath a white baseline spanning about 7% to 73% of slide width. Series: one series, eight bars. Values are not printed; estimates: NBCU about 10.1, Fox about 8.0, TW about 8.0, Disney about 7.8, Netflix about 6.3, Viacom about 5.4, Amazon about 4.5, CBS about 4.2. Highlighted points: Netflix and Amazon bars are orange (#D98228); the other six bars are steel blue (#6C96B2). Legend: none. Bars are evenly spaced, about 3.3% of slide width each, with flat tops and no outlines.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate grey #40484F. Dark charcoal slide with a flat background. Title in white (#FFFFFF) ProximaNova-Light, sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in amber-yellow (#FCB316) ProximaNova-Light, about 4.0% of slide height. Chart tick labels in white (#FFFFFF) ProximaNova-Regular, about 3.0% of slide height. Bars steel blue #6C996B2 variant: #6C96B2 with orange #D98228 accents on Netflix and Amazon. Baseline is a thin white line. Footer text is small, about 1% of slide height, light grey-white. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners or decorative shapes.

Vertical bar chart. Title: none inside the chart; slide title reads More interesting: what is TV?. Series (values about, estimated from bar heights since none are printed): Youtube Games about 249 (orange, highlighted), Twitch about 139 (orange, highlighted), Netflix about 136 (blue), HBO about 133 (blue), ESPN about 86 (blue). Accent colours: orange #D98228 for highlighted bars, steel blue #6C96B2 for other bars. Bars are square-cornered with no shadow. Baseline is a thin white line.

Furious 8

©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 71
Source: Rockstar, boxofficemojo

**Layout**

Single-slide chart layout on a full dark background with a left-aligned title at the top-left (about 3.4% from the left edge, top at 6.1%) and a left-aligned orange subtitle directly beneath it. The vertical bar chart occupies the left and centre of the slide, with the y-axis labels at the left (about 3% from the left edge) and the category axis along the bottom. The footer copyright sits bottom-left at about 3.4% from the left and 95.5% from the top, and the source line sits bottom-right of the chart area at about 75% from the left on the same baseline. No slide number is shown as a separate element; the footer reads Page 71. The right fifth of the slide is empty.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart, single series, no chart title beyond the slide subtitle 'Record global opening weekend sales ($m)'. Y-axis: linear, labelled 0, 200, 400, 600, 800 with unit $m implied by subtitle; axis range 0 to 800; no axis title. X-axis: categorical, five films, labels from left to right: Red Dead Redemption 2, Avengers 3, Fast and the Furious 8, Star Wars 7, Jurassic Park 4. Series values (no numbers printed on the slide, so estimates marked about): Red Dead Redemption 2 about 725 $m (orange bar, highlighted); Avengers 3 about 640 $m; Fast and the Furious 8 about 540 $m; Star Wars 7 about 525 $m; Jurassic Park 4 about 525 $m. Legend: none. Highlighted point: the Red Dead Redemption 2 bar is orange #D98228 while the other four bars are steel blue #6C96B2. Baseline: thin white horizontal axis line at the zero level. No gridlines. Two small raster images sit in the footer area: a footer text image at the bottom left, roughly 194 by 26 pixels, and a source text image at the bottom right, roughly 106 by 27 pixels; a further small image of 86 by 27 pixels and one of 50 by 52 pixels sit in the footer row and show no visible content beyond the footer text.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate colour #40484F. Flat dark slate background with no gradient, texture or photograph. Title and subtitle are set in a light geometric sans-serif (ProximaNova Light). Title is white #FFFFFF at about 5.9% of slide height, subtitle is amber #FCB316 at about 4.0% of slide height. Axis tick labels and category labels are white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Regular at about 3.0% of slide height. Bars are flat fills with square corners and no shadow: orange #D98228 for the highlighted bar and steel blue #6C96B2 for the rest. Baseline is a thin white rule. Footer and source text are small, roughly 1.5% of slide height, light grey-white, ProximaNova Regular.

### Slide 73

**Text**

E-sports are big… / Annual viewers, 2017 (m) / 300 / 200 / 100 / 0 / E-Sports / NFL / NBA / MLB / NHL / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 72 / Source: NewZoo, Goldman Sachs

Single chart slide with no visible grid or columns. The title sits top-left at about x 3.4%, y 6%, with the orange subtitle directly beneath it at y 13%. The bar chart occupies the left and centre of the slide, from about x 3% to 74%, with its baseline at about y 81%. Vertical bar chart. Title: none inside the chart; the slide title and subtitle sit above it. Y-axis: 0 to 300 annual viewers in millions, with labelled ticks at 0, 100, 200 and 300; no axis title. X-axis: categories E-Sports, NFL, NBA, MLB, NHL, with a thin white horizontal baseline. Single series, orange bars, no data labels printed. Estimated values, marked as about: E-Sports about 147m; NFL about 259m; NBA about 239m; MLB about 115m; NHL about 59m. No legend. No highlighted points; all bars share the same colour. Bars are flat rectangles with square corners, evenly spaced, of equal width (about 105 px at 2000 px wide).

Background: Solid slate grey #40484F covering the full slide. Flat dark slate background. Axis and category labels in ProximaNova-Regular, white #FFFFFF, size about 3% of slide height. Bars filled orange #D98228 with no outline, no shadow, no gradient. Baseline is a thin white line. ### Slide 74

**Text**

But the money is not, yet / Annual viewers and revenue, 2017 / 300 / 15 / Annual viewers (m) / Revenue (RH, $bn) / 200 / 10 / 100 / 5 / 0 / 0 / E-Sports / NFL / NBA / MLB / NHL / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 73 / Source: NewZoo, Goldman Sachs

**Layout**

Single-slide chart layout on a full dark-grey field. Title and subtitle sit top-left, left-aligned at about x 3.4% and y 6% to 17%, with the chart filling the middle band from about y 24% to 83%. The left value axis reads 0 to 300 at the far left, the right value axis reads 0 to 15 near the right edge, and the legend sits top-right at x 80%. The footer copyright is bottom-left at y 95.5% and the source line is bottom-right at x 80.4%; there is no slide-number graphic beyond the 'Page 73' text in the footer.

**Visuals**

Chart: grouped vertical bar chart with two series, titled by the slide subtitle 'Annual viewers and revenue, 2017'. Left y-axis: Annual viewers (m), range 0 to 300, gridlines and tick labels at 0, 100, 200, 300. Right y-axis: Revenue (RH, $bn), range 0 to 15, tick labels at 0, 5, 10, 15. X-axis categories: E-Sports, NFL, NBA, MLB, NHL, with a thin white horizontal baseline at 0. Series 1, Annual viewers (m), orange bars plotted on the left axis: E-Sports about 147, NFL about 260, NBA about 240, MLB about 116, NHL about 59 (values estimated; the slide prints none). Series 2, Revenue (RH, $bn), blue bars plotted on the right axis: E-Sports about 0.6, NFL about 13.9, NBA about 8.0, MLB about 10.0, NHL about 4.5 (values estimated; the slide prints none). Legend top-right: orange square 'Annual viewers (m)', blue square 'Revenue (RH, $bn)'. No highlighted points; all bars share the same flat style with no outlines.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F (dark slate grey) across the full slide. Flat dark slate background with no gradient or texture. Title and tick labels and legend text are white #FFFFFF; subtitle and accent are amber #FCB316; bar series use orange #D98228 and steel blue #6C96B2. Typeface is Proxima Nova (geometric sans-serif); title in Light weight, subtitle in Light, axis and legend text in Regular. Title is about 5.9% of slide height, subtitle about 3.9%, tick and category labels about 3.0%, footer and source text smaller. Bars are solid rectangles with no corner radius, shadow or border; the baseline is a 1px white line.

**Text**

And where is that viewing going?

50
0

2012
2013
2014
2015
2016

©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 74
Source: Zenith

**Layout**

Single-column chart slide with a left-aligned title block at the top left, a subtitle directly beneath it, and a full-width area chart below. The y-axis labels sit at the far left, the x-axis years run along the bottom baseline, and series names are set as direct labels to the right of the plot. The footer carries the copyright and page line at bottom left and the source line at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Stacked area chart with a line overlay, no legend, no highlighted points. Y-axis: 0 to 350 minutes per day, labelled every 50 (0, 50, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300, 350), no unit label on the axis; the subtitle gives the unit as average daily minutes per person. X-axis: calendar years 2011 to 2018, one label per year. Series 1, Desktop internet (bottom stacked area, #6C96B2): about 40 in 2011, about 67 in 2012, about 67 in 2013, about 67 in 2014, about 57 in 2015, about 53 in 2016, about 53 in 2017, about 52 in 2018. Series 2, Mobile internet (stacked on top, #A7C0D1): top edge about 84 in 2011 (about 44 mobile), about 126 in 2012, about 141 in 2013, about 155 in 2014, about 180 in 2015, about 189 in 2016, about 218 in 2017, about 239 in 2018. Series 3, TV (orange line, about #E0822A, roughly 3 px stroke, no fill): about 313 in 2011, about 313 in 2012, about 305 in 2013, about 297 in 2014, about 288 in 2015, about 286 in 2016, about 282 in 2017, about 276 in 2018. Values are estimates read from the plot positions; no values are printed on the slide. Thin white horizontal baseline at 0 running under the years. The areas meet vertically at the 2018 edge, with a hard vertical boundary at the right end of the plot.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat dark slate #40484F covering the whole slide. Dark slate background, flat, no gradient or texture. Title in white ProximaNova Light, sentence case, large; subtitle in amber #FCB316 ProximaNova Light, sentence case. Axis numbers in white ProximaNova Regular; direct series labels in white ProximaNova Light. Chart fills are flat tints of blue: #6C96B2 for desktop and #A7C0D1 for mobile, with an orange #E0822A TV line. Title cap height is about 5.9% of slide height; subtitle about 4.0%; axis labels about 3.0%; series labels about 2.5%. Footer text is small, about 1.2% of slide height, white, in a light sans-serif. No shapes, shadows, corner radii or rounded elements.

### Slide 76

**Text**

Software eats money?
$33bn of
investment in
fintech startups
in H1 2018
Old: price comparisons (lightweight,
information arbitrage)
New: credit scores (information
processing, full stack)
Just scratching the surface
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 75
Source: Deloitte

**Layout**

Single-slide composition with a title at top left, a left-hand circle with text and an arrow pointing right, and a right-hand column of three text blocks. The title sits at x 3.4%, y 6.1%, left-aligned. The circle is on the left at x 4.1% to 28.2%, vertically centred around y 57%. The arrow runs from the circle's right edge toward the right column. The three text blocks share a left edge at x 48.3%, distributed vertically at y 32.6%, 51.4% and 70.1%. The footer sits at y 95.5% with the copyright at the left margin (x 2.9%) and the source line at x 74.8%. No slide number is shown separately; the footer text carries 'Page 75'.

**Visuals**

Three non-photographic elements. (1) A solid orange circle, fill #D98228, about 24% of slide width and 43% of slide height, centred behind the text '$33bn of / investment in / fintech startups / in H1 2018', with no outline and no shadow. (2) A flat block arrow pointing right, fill #7F7F7F (mid grey), with a rectangular shaft about 6.7% tall and a triangular head at the right end; it runs from the right edge of the circle to x about 42.8%, sitting at the circle's vertical centre. It has no outline or shadow. (3) A second orange rectangle, fill #D98228, about 17% wide by 30% tall, sitting inside the circle behind the text block; it is the same colour as the circle so it is not distinct from it. No charts, photographs, logos or icons other than the footer marks described below.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat fill #40484F (dark slate grey) covering the whole slide. Flat, minimal dark-grey background with no gradient or texture. Title in white (#FFFFFF) light geometric sans-serif (ProximaNova-Light style) at about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case, with no bold. Body text is white (#FFFFFF) geometric sans-serif (ProximaNova-Light for the right column, ProximaNova-Regular for the circle label) at about 4.4% of slide height, no bullets, generous line spacing. Accent colour is orange #D98228 for the circle; secondary grey #7F7F7F for the arrow. Footer text is small, light grey-white, about 1.3% of slide height. No rounded corners, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 77

**Text**

What’s the real opportunity?
Global market size, 2017 ($tr)
5

Payments

©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 76
Source: McKinsey, BCG

**Layout**

Single-slide chart layout on a full-bleed dark slate background. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top and 3.4% from the left, with an amber subtitle directly beneath it. A vertical bar chart occupies the left-centre area, from about 4.6% to 57% horizontally and 24% to 88% vertically, with its y-axis labels in a column at x 4.6%. The right half of the slide (x above about 60%) is empty. Footer line at y 95.5%: copyright and page number at left (x 2.9%), source line at right (x 74.8%).

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart, three visible bars (Payments, Retail banking, Insurance) plus a category label 'Housing' present in the file with no bar visible. Title of chart: 'Global market size, 2017 ($tr)' (shown as subtitle). Y-axis: labelled 0 to 5 at intervals of 1, no unit label beyond the $tr in the subtitle, range 0 to 5. X-axis: categories as listed, no axis title. Values are not printed; estimates: Payments about 2.2, Retail banking about 2.5, Insurance about 4.6. Bars are a single series in steel blue #6C96B2, equal width (about 6.7% of slide width), evenly spaced, bottoms on a thin white baseline at y 81% spanning x 7.2% to 56.7%. No legend. No highlighted points. Other visuals: none beyond the footer images.

**Design**

Background solid slate #40484F. Title and axis numerals and category labels white #FFFFFF. Subtitle and accent colour amber #FCB316. Bars steel blue #6C96B2. Typeface is Proxima Nova (geometric humanist sans-serif); title in Light weight, axis labels in Regular weight. Title cap height about 5.9% of slide height (font size 5.93%), subtitle 3.95%, axis labels 2.96%, footer text small. Bars have square corners, no shadow, no gradient. Baseline is a thin white line about 0.2% high. No borders on bars.

**Layout**

Single-column left-aligned title block with a two-column split: a stacked-bar chart occupies the left half (about 3% to 41% of width) and a text block sits in the right half starting at about 52% of width. The title is at top-left (x 3.4%, y 6.1%) with a yellow subtitle directly beneath it; the footer runs along the bottom edge at about y 95.5% with the copyright text at left and the source line at about 36% of width; no slide number is shown apart from the 'Page 78' text inside the footer.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart titled 'Global population, 2017' (subtitle). Y-axis runs 0% to 100% with labelled ticks at 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, 100% (no units beyond %); bars are not labelled with numeric values. Two columns on a horizontal baseline at about y 81%: 'USA' (x about 13% to 19.5%) and 'Global' (x about 29% to 35.4%). USA column: banked segment (#6C96B2) from 0% to about 93%, unbanked segment (#D98228) from about 93% to 100%, so about 93% banked and about 7% unbanked (estimate, no value printed). Global column: banked segment (#6C96B2) from 0% to about 69%, unbanked segment (#D98228) from about 69% to 100%, so about 69% banked and about 31% unbanked (estimate, no value printed). Segment labels are printed in white inside the Global column only: 'Unbanked' centred in the orange segment and 'Banked' centred in the blue segment. No legend is shown; no points are highlighted. Baseline is a thin white horizontal line. Source line and copyright are small text images in the footer, not logos. A small 55 by 52 pixel image sits at x 14.8%, y 95.5% overlapping the end of the footer copyright text and is not visible at this scale. No photographs, icons or logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F covering the whole slide. Dark slate background (#40484F) with no gradient or texture. Chart axis labels and body text in ProximaNova Regular, white #FFFFFF, body about 4.4% of slide height and axis labels about 3.0%. Data segment labels about 2.5% in white. Bars are flat fills with no outline, no shadows, no rounded corners: banked blue #6C96B2 and unbanked orange #D98228. Baseline is a thin white rule. Footer text is very small (about 1.2% of slide height) in white.

**Layout**

Two side-by-side bar charts in two equal columns below a full-width title block, each chart with its heading left-aligned above it at the left edge of its column. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge. Footer line runs along the bottom at about 95.5% height, with the copyright and page number at the left, a source line under the left chart and another at the right edge.

**Visuals**

Two vertical bar charts. Left chart: type bar chart, title 'Mobile payment penetration, 2018', y-axis 0% to 100% in 25% steps (ticks labelled 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, 100%), x-axis categories USA and China, no unit label beyond %. Series: USA bar about 22% (blue, no printed value); China bar about 81% (orange, no printed value). Right chart: type bar chart, title 'Mobile payment value, 2017 ($tr)', y-axis 0 to 14 in steps of 2 (ticks labelled 0, 2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14), with a rotated label reading 'Thousands' at the left of the axis (not legible in the render), x-axis categories USA and China. Series: USA bar about 0.1 (blue, no printed value, barely visible); China bar about 12.8 (orange, no printed value). No legend. No highlighted points; the colour difference separates USA (blue) from China (orange). Both baselines are thin white horizontal lines.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F. Flat dark slate background with no gradient, texture or photograph. Titles and tick labels are white #FFFFFF in Proxima Nova Light for the title and Proxima Nova Regular for axis labels, sans-serif geometric. Chart subheadings are amber #FCB316 in Proxima Nova Light. Bars are flat fills with square corners and no outline: USA blue #6C96B2, China orange #D98228. Axis baselines are white. Title text is about 5.9% of slide height, subheadings about 4.0%, tick and category labels about 3.0%, footer text about 1.2%. No shadows.

### Slide 82

**Text**

New kinds of solution
Vastly more / data
+
Different / consumer / expectations
+
Unbundling
+
Machine / learning
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 81

**Layout**

Single-slide content layout with the title at top left, a horizontal row of four equal circles vertically centred in the middle band of the slide, grey plus signs between adjacent circles, and a small copyright and page line at bottom left. The title sits left-aligned at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6% from the top. Margins are about 3% left and right, and the circle row spans from about 2.9% to 96% of the width. There is no slide number element separate from the footer line, which reads 'Page 81'.

**Visuals**

Four flat-filled circles in a single row, each about 18.1% of slide width and 32.2% of slide height: a steel-blue circle #6C96B2 labelled 'Vastly more data', an orange circle #D98228 labelled 'Different consumer expectations', a magenta circle #BE4681 labelled 'Unbundling', and a lime-green circle #95CA53 labelled 'Machine learning'. Between each pair of circles sits a bold grey plus sign #BFBFBF, about 3% of slide width and 6% of slide height, centred vertically on the circles. The copyright line is a small raster image of text, 194 by 26 pixels. A second tiny raster image of 51 by 52 pixels sits at about x 14.8%, y 95.5%, but shows no discernible content at this size. No charts, photographs or logos apart from the footer line.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #40484F (dark slate grey) covering the entire slide. Flat dark slate background #40484F with no gradient, texture or pattern. Title is white #FFFFFF in Proxima Nova Light, a geometric sans-serif, at about 5.9% of slide height. Circle labels are white #FFFFFF in Proxima Nova Regular, sans-serif, at about 3.5% of slide height, centred and set on two or three lines. Accents are the four circle fills #6C96B2, #D98228, #BE4681 and #95CA53 and grey plus signs #BFBFBF. Circles have no outline, no shadow and no corner radius because they are true circles. Plus signs are solid with square ends. No shadows or gradients anywhere. Footer is small light grey text, about 1.3% of slide height.

### Slide 83

**Text**

Software eats death?
Drug discovery
$75bn
Machine learning transforms discovery
Social and wearables transform trials
Programming genes and cells means new
kinds of cure
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 82

**Layout**

Single-column title at top left, with the title's left edge at about 3.4% of slide width and its top at about 6.1% of slide height. Below it, a large orange circle sits on the left third, followed by a grey right-pointing block arrow, and three left-aligned statements form a column starting at about 48.3% of slide width. The footer sits bottom left at about 3% from the left edge and about 95.5% down the slide, with the page number printed after the copyright text.

**Visuals**

A flat orange filled circle, centred at about x 16%, y 57%, about 24% wide and 43% tall, colour #D98228, containing the white text 'Drug discovery' above '$75bn'. To its right, a flat grey block arrow pointing right, about 15.6% wide and 6.7% tall, colour #7F7F7F, with its shaft and head vertically centred on the circle's midpoint at about y 57%; the arrow starts at the circle's right edge and ends at about x 43%. Two small image pieces at the footer (about 12.9% by 3.1% and 1.9% by 3.1%) carry the rasterised copyright and page-number text in white, with no logo. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F. Dark slate background #40484F with no gradient or texture. Title and all body text are white #FFFFFF. The only accent is the orange #D98228 circle; the arrow is neutral grey #7F7F7F. Typeface is Proxima Nova, a geometric sans-serif: Light for the title and the right-hand statements, Regular for 'Drug discovery' and '$75bn'. Title is about 5.9% of slide height; body text is about 4.4% of slide height. Shapes have no outline, shadow or corner radius (the circle is a true ellipse, the arrow a plain block arrow).

**Layout**

Single-column chart slide on a dark grey field. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6% from the top, with a yellow subtitle directly beneath it. A vertical bar chart spans from about x 4% to x 73% and y 24% to 89%, with the y-axis labels at the left of the plot and the category labels under the baseline. The footer has a copyright line at bottom-left (about x 3%, y 95.5%) and a source line at bottom-right of the chart area (about x 75%, y 95.5%). There is no slide number outside the footer text and no logo.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart, untitled apart from the slide subtitle 'Annual spending, 2017 ($tr)'. Y-axis labelled 0, 2, 4, 6, 8 (units $tr, range 0 to 8), with no axis title. X-axis categories from left to right: Drug discovery, Drug market, Global health insurance, US healthcare, Global healthcare. Single series with labelled values: Drug discovery $75bn (printed label, about 0.075 on the axis, rendered as a thin orange bar); Drug market about 0.5 (no printed value); Global health insurance about 1.0 (no printed value); US healthcare about 3.5 (no printed value); Global healthcare $7.2tr (printed label above the bar). Bars are #6C96B2 steel blue, except the Drug discovery bar which is orange (about #E8862A) and highlighted. No legend. The baseline is a thin white horizontal line at the zero level running from about x 7% to x 73%.

Subtitle in amber-yellow (#FCB316) Proxima Nova Light, about 4% of slide height. Axis numbers and category labels in white (#FFFFFF) Proxima Nova Regular, about 3% of slide height. Value labels in white Proxima Nova Light, about 3.5% of slide height. Footer text in white at small size, about 2.5% of slide height. Bars are flat with square corners, no shadows, no outlines. Background #40484F.

### Slide 85

**Text**

More interesting: software changes what bio can address / $75bn / $7.2tr / ? / Creation / New ways to / discover drugs / Diagnosis / Diagnose at / earlier stages, / saving lives and / money / Prevention / Whole-life / tracking, / management and / health / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 84

**Layout**

Three circles in a horizontal row across the middle of the slide, connected by two grey right-pointing arrows, each circle with a figure above it. Title sits top-left, left-aligned at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6% from the top, on one line. Footer copyright and page number sit bottom-left at about 3% from the left edge and 95.5% from the top. Margins are roughly 3% left and right; the circles are centred on x of about 14%, 50% and 83%.

**Visuals**

Diagram: three outlined circles of equal size (about 25% of slide width, 40% of slide height each), left to right. Circle 1 has an orange stroke (about #E08A2A, roughly 0.4% of slide height) with label 'Creation' in bold and body text 'New ways to / discover drugs', with figure '$75bn' printed above it. Circle 2 has a steel-blue stroke (about #6A9DBA) with 'Diagnosis' in bold and body text 'Diagnose at / earlier stages, / saving lives and / money', with figure '$7.2tr' above it. Circle 3 has a magenta-pink stroke (about #C94A86) with 'Prevention' in bold and body text 'Whole-life / tracking, / management and / health', with figure '?' above it. Between circle 1 and 2, and between circle 2 and 3, is a solid light grey (#BEBEBE) right-pointing block arrow, about 6.5% of slide width by 7% of slide height, with a rectangular shaft and triangular head. Circles are unfilled, showing the background through them. No chart axes, no legend, no photographs. Footer contains a small white logo image (194 by 26 px) reading the copyright text, and a second small image (56 by 52 px) at x 14.8, which is not separately legible.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate grey #40484F. Dark slate background #40484F. Titles and figures in white (#FFFFFF) ProximaNova Light sans-serif, title about 5.9% of slide height, not bold, sentence case. Circle headings in ProximaNova Bold white, about 4.4% of slide height. Body text in ProximaNova Regular white about 3.95% of slide height, centred. Figures in ProximaNova Light white about 4.4% of slide height. Accent colours on circle strokes are orange, steel blue and magenta. Arrows light grey #BEBEBE. Line strokes about 0.4% of slide height, no shadows, no gradients, no corner radii (circles are perfect circles).

Organising the internet

**Layout**

Single slide with a title at top left, aligned to a left margin of about 3.4% of the width. Three circles sit in one horizontal row across the vertical middle, joined by two block arrows. The footer line sits at bottom left, and the page number '87' sits at the end of the footer. The right fifth of the slide is covered by a dark panel that hides content placed beneath it.

**Visuals**

Three circles and two block arrows form a left-to-right flow diagram. Circle 1 is a solid blue-grey circle (#6C96B2) labelled 'AOL', '‘Information superhighway’' and 'Interactive TV'. Arrow 1 is a solid grey right block arrow (about #7F8A96) pointing right from circle 1 to circle 2. Circle 2 is an outlined circle with a white dashed stroke (about 0.3% of slide height wide), no fill, labelled 'Internet and the web'. Arrow 2 is an identical grey right block arrow pointing right from circle 2 to circle 3. Circle 3 is a solid orange circle (#D98228) labelled 'Google' and 'Facebook'. No photographs or logos are visible. The footer image at bottom left (194 by 26 px) carries the copyright line '©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 87'. A small 55 by 52 px image sits beside it. Hidden beneath the dark right-hand panel are a 150 by 71 px image and the text 'Crypto…', which are not visible on the rendered slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F covering the whole slide. Dark slate background (#40484F). Titles are white (#FFFFFF) in ProximaNova Light, a geometric sans-serif, with a title cap height of about 5.9% of slide height. Body labels are white ProximaNova Regular at about 3.5% of slide height. Circle fills are blue-grey #6C96B2 and orange #D98228, with white text. Arrows are flat grey block arrows (about #7F8A96). The dashed circle has a white dashed outline. No shadows, gradients or corner radii are visible. Footer text is small white sans-serif.

### Slide 89

**Text**

Organising the internet / Google / Facebook / Centralised / Capture intent, meaning and value / Highly abstracted / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 88

**Layout**

Single-column title at top left with a left-aligned title starting at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6% from the top. Below it, a large orange circle sits on the left half with a grey arrow leaving its right edge, and three left-aligned statements form a column starting at about 48% of the slide width. The copyright footer sits bottom left at about 3% from the left edge and 95.5% from the top, with the page number 88 at its end.

**Visuals**

Diagram: one solid orange circle (#D98228, about 24% of slide width by 43% of slide height, centred at about x 16%, y 57%) labelled 'Google' on the first line and 'Facebook' on the second line, both in white. A flat grey (#7F7F7F) block arrow starts at the circle's right edge and points right; the shaft is about 6.7% of slide height thick and the arrowhead is a triangle about 15.6% of slide width long, ending at about x 43%. The arrow is vertically centred on the circle at about y 57%. Three text statements sit to the right of the arrow with no connecting lines: 'Centralised', 'Capture intent, meaning and value', 'Highly abstracted'. Footer: a small raster line of text '©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 88' (about 12.9% of slide width wide, 3.1% of slide height tall) and a second tiny raster image (about 1.9% by 3.1%) at x 14.8% overlapping the end of the footer line.

**Design**

Background: Flat solid slate colour #40484F covering the whole slide. Flat dark slate background (#40484F) with no gradient, texture or photograph. Title, body text and footer text are white (#FFFFFF). The only accent is the orange circle (#D98228) and the grey arrow (#7F7F7F). Typeface is Proxima Nova: a geometric humanist sans-serif, Light weight for the title and statements and Regular weight for the words inside the circle and the footer. Title text is about 5.9% of slide height and body text about 4.4% of slide height. Shapes have no outline, corner radius, shadow or gradient.

### Slide 90

**Text**

Organising the internet / AOL / ‘Information / superhighway’ / Interactive TV / Internet and / the web / Google / Facebook / Machine / learning / Crypto / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 89

**Layout**

Single row of four circles spanning the slide width, vertically centred in the middle band, connected left to right by three grey block arrows. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top and 3.4% from the left, left-aligned. The copyright footer sits bottom-left at about 95.5% from the top, with the Andreessen Horowitz logo at its left edge followed by the page number.

**Visuals**

Four circles and three arrows, forming a left-to-right flow diagram. Circle 1 (left): solid filled circle in steel blue #6C96B2 with white text AOL, ‘Information superhighway’, Interactive TV. Circle 2: dashed white outline circle, no fill (background shows through), with white text Internet and the web. Circle 3: solid filled circle in orange #D98228 with white text Google, Facebook. Circle 4 (right): dashed white outline circle, no fill, with white text Machine learning, Crypto. Three arrows: solid grey right-pointing block arrows (about #838D98) placed between each pair of circles, each connecting the right edge of one circle to the left edge of the next. Footer logo: Andreessen Horowitz wordmark (194 by 26 px) in white, followed by a small square logo mark (57 by 52 px) in white, both at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background dark slate grey #40484F, flat, no gradient. Titles white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Light (geometric sans-serif), title size about 5.9% of slide height. Body text white #FFFFFF in ProximaNova Regular, about 3.5% of slide height, centred. Accents: steel blue #6C96B2 and orange #D98228 circle fills; dashed outline circles in white, about 0.5% of slide height stroke width with evenly spaced dashes; arrows grey #838D98 solid block arrows with no outline. No shadows, no corner radii (circles are perfect circles), no gradients.

Two-panel split: a dark slate panel fills the left 35% of the slide and a light grey panel the right 65%. The title sits left-aligned in the left panel at about 48% of slide height, with the footer line at bottom left (about 3% margin, about 95.5% from top). The chair photograph is centred in the right panel, with its foot shadow at about 86% of slide height. No slide number is shown apart from the 'Page 90' in the footer.

**Visuals**

One photograph: a black-stained wooden wishbone chair (Y-shaped back with a curved top rail, straight tapered legs, two horizontal stretchers, and a natural woven paper-cord seat in pale beige) shown in front view, centred on a seamless light grey backdrop. A soft grey shadow sits under the chair. The chair spans roughly 55% to 78% of slide width and 29% to 86% of slide height. Lighting is soft and even from the front, with no strong highlights. Dominant colours: near-black wood #1E1E1E, natural cord #D9C9A8, backdrop #F5F5F5. The photo is not cropped and sits inside the right panel without a border. Its left edge lies under the dark panel, which is not visible there. Footer image: the copyright and page text rendered as a small image.

**Design**

Background: Split background: flat slate #40484F on the left 35% and flat light grey #F5F5F5 on the right 65%. Left panel is flat dark slate #40484F. Right panel and photo backdrop are light grey #F5F5F5 (measured fill #FFFFFF underneath the photo). Title is white #FFFFFF in Proxima Nova Light, a clean geometric sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height, regular case with no letter-spacing. Footer is Proxima Nova Regular in white #FFFFFF at about 1.2% of slide height. Shapes have square corners, no outlines and no shadows; the only shadow is the photographed floor shadow of the chair.

**Layout**

Single slide on a dark slate background with a left-aligned title across the top-left at about 3% margin. Three equal circles sit in one horizontal row, vertically centred on the slide, with two grey block arrows between them pointing right. The footer copyright line sits bottom-left at about 3% margin with no slide number element apart from the printed 'Page 92'.

**Visuals**

Three filled circles and two block arrows, no photographs or logos in the content area. Circle 1 is light steel blue #6C96B2 with the text 'It’s a chair!'. Circle 2 is orange #D98228 with the text 'It’s a modern Scandinavian design classic'. Circle 3 is magenta-pink #BE4681 with the text 'OK, we won’t show you any more Disney Cruises ads'. Each circle is about 24.9% of slide width and 44.3% of slide height, so the circles are true circles, spaced about 7% apart horizontally. Between circles 1 and 2, and between circles 2 and 3, is a right-pointing block arrow filled #85929C, about 5.0% wide and 12.8% tall, with a rectangular shaft and triangular head, vertically centred on the circles. Bottom-left is a small copyright image and a tiny image fragment forming the footer line; these are the footer text rendered as images and are not decorative.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate #40484F. Dark slate background #40484F with no gradient or texture. Title and all body text are white #FFFFFF. Accents are the three circle fills: steel blue #6C96B2, orange #D98228 and magenta #BE4681, with arrows in grey #85929C. Title is set in ProximaNova-Light, a geometric sans-serif, at about 5.9% of slide height, which is a large heading. Body text in the circles is ProximaNova-Regular, a geometric sans-serif, at about 4.4% of slide height, centred. Shapes have flat fills with no outline, no shadow, no rounded corners beyond the circle geometry, and no gradients.

Machine learning as tools to scale people
Scaling people
“A million interns to look / at a million pictures”
Superpower
"One intern looks at a / million pictures"
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 94

**Layout**

Single title line at top left with a left margin of about 3.4% and top about 6%, followed by two large circles of equal size placed side by side in the lower two-thirds, centred horizontally in left and right halves, joined by a double-headed dotted arrow at vertical centre. Footer credit line bottom left with logo images at bottom left; no slide number graphic beyond the printed 'Page 94'.

**Visuals**

Two outlined circles and one double-headed dotted arrow. Left circle: orange outline (about #D98025), stroke about 0.3% of slide height, unfilled, centred near x 30%, y 49%, diameter about 50% of slide height, containing bold heading 'Scaling people' and quotation 'A million interns to look at a million pictures'. Right circle: steel-blue outline (about #5B8DB0), same stroke weight and size, unfilled, centred near x 74%, y 49%, containing bold heading 'Superpower' and quotation 'One intern looks at a million pictures'. Between the circles a grey (#A8ADB2) dotted line with filled triangular arrowheads at both ends, spanning from the left circle's right edge to the right circle's left edge at vertical centre y about 57%. Footer carries two small logo images: a wide white wordmark (194 by 26 px) reading the Andreessen Horowitz logo and a small square icon (56 by 52 px) at bottom left.

**Design**

Background dark slate grey #40484F, flat with no gradient. Title in ProximaNova Light (geometric sans-serif, light weight), white #FFFFFF, about 5.9% of slide height. Circle headings in ProximaNova Bold, white #FFFFFF, about 4.4% of slide height. Quotations in ProximaNova Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 4.0% of slide height, centred, two lines each. Accent colours: orange #D98025 for left circle outline, steel blue #5B8DB0 for right circle outline, grey #A8ADB2 for the arrow. Footer credit text small, about 1.2% of slide height, light grey-white. Circles have no fill, no shadow, no rounded corners beyond circular form.

### Slide 96

**Text**

Crypto? / ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 95

**Layout**

Two-column split layout. A dark slate panel fills the left 35% of the slide full height, holding a single left-aligned title placed at about the vertical middle (top of the title at 47.8% of height) with a 3.4% left margin. The right 65% is white and holds one cut-out photograph of an Enigma-style cipher machine, centred in that area with generous white space. The footer sits bottom-left on the dark panel at about 95.5% from the top, with no slide number element beyond the printed 'Page 95'.

**Visuals**

One photograph, placed as a cut-out with no background, on the white right-hand panel. Subject: a cipher machine resembling an Enigma, seen from the front-left at a raised three-quarter angle. Its casing is black-brown, slightly textured metal. The top has a perforated grid of about 26 round holes with rows of brass-coloured lamp-style keys, a brass bar and hinge at the upper left, and three dark slotted rotor covers. The front panel has a row of letter labels (visible letters include W, E, R, T, Z, U, I, O, Q, G, H, B, F, Y, X, P, K, J, L) above tiny brass-ringed sockets and dark oval switch caps. Several brown braided cables trail from the front and loop across the bottom. Composition: object fills roughly the central 53% of the slide width and 82% of its height, not cropped, with the full machine visible. Lighting: soft even studio light with mild highlights on the brass parts. Dominant colours: near-black brown #2B2622 for the casing, brass #B5A27A for fittings, white #FFFFFF background. No overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Split background: solid slate #40484F covering the left 35% and solid white #FFFFFF covering the right 65%. Dark slate left panel #40484F with a plain white right panel #FFFFFF; no gradients, shadows, borders or corner radii. Title in Proxima Nova Light, white #FFFFFF, sentence case, about 5.9% of slide height. Footer in Proxima Nova Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 1.2% of slide height. No accent colour is used. The photograph has no frame.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 35, h 100 [#40484F]: Left dark panel, plain rectangle (Solid fill, no outline, no shadow, square corners, full slide height, sits behind the title and footer.)
- shape at x 35, y 0, w 65, h 100 [#FFFFFF]: Right white panel, plain rectangle (Solid fill, no outline, square corners, sits behind the photograph.)
- photo at x 42.1, y 15.3, w 53.1, h 81.5 [#2B2622]: Photograph of an Enigma-style cipher machine, cut out on white, not cropped, no frame, no overlay (Source image 765 by 660 pixels; object shown in full with its natural cut-out edge; no border, shadow or rounded corners; sits on the white right panel and does not overlap the dark panel.)
- title at x 3.4, y 47.8, w 11.2, h 5.9 [Proxima Nova Light, sans-serif, regular weight, sentence case, no italics, no letter-spacing change; font size 5.93% of slide height; #FFFFFF; left aligned]: Crypto? (Single line, sits on the slate panel.)
- footer at x 2.9, y 95.5, w 13.8, h 3.1 [Proxima Nova Regular, sans-serif, regular weight, sentence case; font size 1.2% of slide height; #FFFFFF; left aligned]: ©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. ### Slide 98

**Text**

What applications?
Early share in tools and application layers did not last
100%
WWW, 3%
Gopher
75%
Other
E-Mail
Usenet
50%
Linux, 9%
Windows
Telnet
25%
Mac
0%
FTP
Web servers, 1995
Internet traffic, 1993
Sun
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 97
Source: MSDW, Microsoft

**Layout**

Single-slide chart layout on a full-bleed dark slate background. The title sits top-left at about 3.4% from the left edge and 6.1% from the top, with the orange subtitle directly beneath it. Two stacked columns sit on a shared white baseline spanning roughly x 8% to 74%, with the y-axis percentage labels in a column at the left, and the footer copyright line at bottom-left and source line at bottom-right, with no slide number element beyond the footer text.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart. Title: none inside the chart area; slide title and subtitle are above it. Y-axis: percentage share, 0% to 100%, labelled every 25% (0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, 100%), no units beyond %. X-axis categories: 'Web servers, 1995' (left column) and 'Internet traffic, 1993' (right column). Left column 'Web servers, 1995' segments bottom to top: Sun about 0% to 30% (orange #D98228), Mac about 30% to 47% (blue #6C96B2), Windows about 47% to 62% (magenta #BE4681), Linux 9% (green #95CA53, labelled 'Linux, 9%' with a leader line from the segment's right edge), Other about 71% to 100% (yellow #FCB316, about 29%). Right column 'Internet traffic, 1993' segments bottom to top: FTP about 0% to 41% (orange #D98228), Telnet about 41% to 66% (blue #6C96B2), Usenet about 66% to 81% (magenta #BE4681), E-Mail about 81% to 93% (green #95CA53), Gopher about 93% to 97% (yellow #FCB316, label printed inside the segment), WWW 3% (purple #7A3F9D, labelled 'WWW, 3%' with a leader line to the right). Segment labels are printed inside each segment in white. Legend: none; series are identified by the in-segment labels. Highlighted points: none. Columns are flat-filled rectangles with no outlines, no gradients and no shadows. Footer images: the copyright line and source line are rendered as small text images.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate colour #40484F covering the entire slide. Dark slate background #40484F with all text in white #FFFFFF except the subtitle in amber #FCB316. Title and body typefaces are Proxima Nova (light for title and subtitle, regular for labels), a geometric sans-serif. Title is about 5.9% of slide height, subtitle about 4.0%, axis labels about 3.0%, segment and annotation labels about 2.5%. Column segments are flat colour blocks with square corners and no shadows. Baseline is a thin white line about 0.2% high. Leader lines are thin white strokes.

### Slide 100

**Text**

Organising the internet
AOL
‘Information superhighway’
Interactive TV
Internet and the web
Google
Facebook
Machine learning
Crypto
©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 99

**Layout**

Four circular nodes sit in a single horizontal row across the middle of the slide, joined left to right by three block arrows, with the title at top left and a small footer at bottom left. The title and footer share a left margin of about 3% of the slide width; the row is centred vertically between about 39% and 72% of the slide height.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a left-to-right process chain of four circles connected by three grey block arrows. Circle 1 (left) is a solid light steel-blue (#6C96B2) filled circle labelled 'AOL', ‘Information superhighway’ and 'Interactive TV' in white. Circle 2 is an outlined circle with no fill and a white dashed stroke, labelled 'Internet and the web'. Circle 3 is a solid orange (#D98228) filled circle labelled 'Google' and 'Facebook' in white. Circle 4 (right) is an outlined circle with no fill and a white dashed stroke, labelled 'Machine learning' and 'Crypto'. Three arrows are solid block arrows with a rectangular shaft and triangular head, filled grey (about #818B96), pointing right, each placed in the gap between adjacent circles and not touching them. The footer is a small raster image of the copyright line. No photographs, charts, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat dark slate grey #40484F covering the whole slide. Dark slate background with flat, unadorned shapes; no gradients, shadows or corner radii (circles are perfect circles). Title in white Proxima Nova Light, sentence case, large (about 5.9% of slide height). Node labels in white Proxima Nova Regular, centred, about 3.5% of slide height, with line spacing about 1.5 lines between entries. Accent colours are steel blue #6C96B2 and orange #D98228; arrows are grey #818B96; dashed outlines are white, roughly 0.4% of slide height stroke width, with dash lengths of about 1.5% of slide width. Typefaces are geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova).

**Text**

©2018 Andreessen Horowitz. Page 102

**Layout**

Two-zone composition on a dark slate field: a square-framed photograph sits in the upper-left-centre area with a single quotation line centred beneath it, left edge near x 20%. The right half of the slide is empty. No title. The footer sits bottom-left at about 3% margin with the copyright and page number on one line.

**Visuals**

One photograph: a candid, slightly soft colour snapshot of a smiling man with short brown hair, wearing a dark charcoal crew-neck sweater over a white collar, turned three-quarters toward the camera. Behind him is a city street: a pinkish-brown multi-storey building with rectangular windows, a dark awning with wooden-looking beams at upper left, a red minivan, a dark car, a white-and-blue city bus, and a yellow taxi. Lighting is daylight, flat and slightly overcast. Dominant colours are dusty pink-brown, grey asphalt, charcoal and red. The image is a plain rectangle, not cropped to a shape, with no overlay or tint. No logos or icons.

**Design**

Background solid slate grey #40484F, flat with no gradient or pattern. Quotation text white #FFFFFF in a light geometric humanist sans-serif (ProximaNova Light), roughly 4.4% of slide height, no capitals, no italics. Footer text light grey #C9CED3 in a small sans-serif regular weight at about 1.6% of slide height. Photograph is an unrounded rectangle with no border or shadow. No accent colours, rules or shapes other than the photo.
